政策内互动何时有帮助？基于价值的模仿学习中的代表性权衡When Does On-Policy Interaction Help? Representational Tradeoffs in Value-Based Imitation Learning

卢卡·维亚诺Luca Viano安托万·穆兰Antoine Moulin奥黛丽·黄 Volkan Cevher luca.viano@epfl.ch antoine.moulin@upf.edu audreyh5@illinois.edu volkan.cevher@epfl.chAudrey Huang Volkan Cevher luca.viano@epfl.ch antoine.moulin@upf.edu audreyh5@illinois.edu volkan.cevher@epfl.ch

volkan.cevher@epfl.ch

菲利普·阿莫蒂拉 p.amortila@berkeley.eduPhilip Amortila p.amortila@berkeley.edu

迪伦·J·福斯特 dylanfoster@microsoft.comDylan J. Foster dylanfoster@microsoft.com

摘要Abstract

模仿学习（IL）——训练代理复制演示中的专家行为——支撑着从机器人到语言模型训练的应用。众所周知，诸如行为克隆（BC）之类的标准方法会遭受复合错误和性能停滞的困扰，特别是当学习者无法完美地代表专家的策略时（如典型的情况，例如在蒸馏中）。根据经验，两种干预措施被广泛理解以提高绩效：询问专家Imitation learning (IL)—training an agent to replicate expert behavior from demonstrations—underpins applications from robotics to language model training. Standard approaches such as Behavior Cloning (BC) are known to suffer from compounding errors and performance plateaus, particularly when the learner cannot perfectly represent the expert’s policy (as is typical, e.g., in distillation). Two interventions are widely understood empirically to improve performance: querying the expert交互地interactively沿着学习者自己的轨迹，并在生成策略的过程中使用价值函数估计，而不是直接拟合专家的完整动作分布。along the learner’s own trajectories, and using value function estimation en route to generating a policy rather than directly fitting the expert’s full action distribution.

我们研究了这些改进的本质及其潜在的令人惊讶的相互作用。我们的主要发现是专家互动放松了We investigate the nature of these improvements and their potentially surprising interplay. Our main finding is that expert interaction relaxes the代表性的representational对学习者的要求：只需要一个能够实现专家价值函数的模型，绕过实现专家策略本身的（通常更严格的）要求。具体来说，我们引入了 OVI，一种交互式同策略 IL 算法，只要学习者能够表示专家的价值函数，该算法就具有统计上的效率，并且在访问线性最大化预言机的情况下具有计算上的效率。我们用负面结果来补充这一点，表明互动是必要的。也就是说，如果没有超出专家价值可实现性的更强假设，任何离线 IL 算法都必须根据专家策略类的复杂性进行扩展。我们的研究结果得到了实证的证实。 OVI 的性能优于基于离线策略 (BC)、基于交互式策略 (DAgger) 和基于离线价值的 IL 方法，当学习者网络的表达能力大大低于专家网络时，OVI 的收益最大。demands on the learner: one only needs a model capable of realizing the expert’s value function, bypassing the (often stricter) requirement of realizing the expert’s policy itself. Concretely, we introduce OVI, an interactive on-policy IL algorithm that is statistically efficient whenever the learner can represent the expert’s value function and computationally efficient given access to a linear maximization oracle. We complement this with a negative result showing that interaction is necessary. Namely, without stronger assumptions beyond expert-value realizability alone, any offline IL algorithm must scale with the complexity of the expert policy class. Our findings bear out empirically. OVI outperforms offline policy-based (BC), interactive policy-based (DAgger), and offline value-based IL methods, with the largest gains when the learner network is substantially less expressive than the expert’s.

1 简介1 Introduction

训练一个模型来复制更有能力的专家的行为——这个过程称为Training a model to replicate the behavior of a more capable expert—a process known as模仿学习imitation learning(IL)——已成为现代人工智能的核心范式。在语言建模中，蒸馏训练较小的语言模型（LM）来重现较大语言模型的输出（Agarwal et al., 2024；Lu and Thinking Machines Lab, 2025）；在机器人和自主导航领域，智能体直接从专家演示中学习复杂的操作技能（Osa 等人，2018 年；Pomerleau，1988 年）。即使有充足的离线专家数据，行为克隆（BC，Pomerleau，1991）等主要方法也常常会遇到收敛缓慢、训练不稳定和性能停滞的问题（de Haan 等人，2019；Block 等人，2024；Laskey 等人，2017；Hu 等人，2025；Spencer 等人，2021）。这些缺点通常归因于两个相互关联的现象：沿地平线的错误传播（Ross and Bagnell，2010；Ross et al.，2011），以及学习者无法忠实地捕捉专家的策略（Espinosa-Dice et al.，2025；Rohatgi et al.，2025）。根据经验，通常观察到两种干预措施可以依次缓解这些问题。(IL)—has become a central paradigm in modern AI. In language modeling, distillation trains a smaller language model (LM) to reproduce the outputs of a larger one (Agarwal et al., 2024; Lu and Thinking Machines Lab, 2025); in robotics and autonomous navigation, agents learn complex manipulation skills directly from expert demonstrations (Osa et al., 2018; Pomerleau, 1988). Predominant approaches such as Behavior Cloning (BC, Pomerleau, 1991) often suffer from slow convergence, training instability, and performance plateaus even given ample offline expert data (de Haan et al., 2019; Block et al., 2024; Laskey et al., 2017; Hu et al., 2025; Spencer et al., 2021). These shortcomings are commonly attributed to two interrelated phenomena: error propagation along the horizon (Ross and Bagnell, 2010; Ross et al., 2011), and the inability of the learner to faithfully capture the expert’s policy (Espinosa-Dice et al., 2025; Rohatgi et al., 2025). Two interventions are commonly observed empirically to mitigate each of these problems in turn.

第一个干预措施是The first intervention is that of同策略交互on-policy interaction，即沿着学习者自己的轨迹询问专家的行为。, i.e., the act of querying the expert along the learner’s own trajectories.互动Interactive（或(or在政策on-policy) IL 被理解为通过学习按策略纠正错误来避免错误放大。这种好处通常是通过改善视野依赖性来形式化的（Ross 等人，2011；Rajaraman 等人，2021a）。然而，福斯特等人最近的工作。 （2024）指出，当学习者能够准确地表达专家策略时，BC 和交互式 IL 之间的明显差距就会消失，使得交互的精确作用不像以前看起来那么清晰。) IL is understood to avoid error amplification by learning to correct mistakes on-policy. This benefit has classically been formalized through improved horizon dependence (Ross et al., 2011; Rajaraman et al., 2021a). However, recent work by Foster et al. (2024) establishes that when the learner can accurately represent the expert policy, the apparent gap between BC and interactive IL disappears, making the precise role of interaction less clear than it once seemed.

We compare with offline and interactive policy-based methods (BC; Pomerleau, 1988; DAgger; Ross et al., 2011), and the offline value-based method SPOIL (Moulin et al., 2025a).当学习者网络缩小时，OVI 的性能优于以下每种方法：（1）基于离线价值和（2）基于交互策略——从而支持该理论When the learner network shrinks, OVI outperforms each of the following methods alone: (1) offline value-based and (2) interactive policy-based—thereby supporting the theory这预示着代表性收益只能通过专家互动和基于价值的设计共同产生。更多详细信息，请参见第 3.3 节。that predicts that representational gains can emerge only from expert interaction and value-based design jointly. See Section 3.3 for more details.

第二个并行干预是A second, parallel intervention is基于价值的value-basedIL，估计IL, where an estimated价值或奖励函数value or reward function用于辅助政策学习（Garg et al., 2021; Swamy et al., 2021, 2022a），包含大多数逆强化学习（IRL）和学徒学习方法（Abbeel and Ng, 2004; Syed and Schapire, 2007; Ho and Ermon, 2016）。基于价值的 IL 不是拟合专家对行动的完整分布，包括具有同等价值的潜在任意或外生选择，而是寻求仅恢复足够的信息来匹配专家的回报。这提出了基于价值的学习者能够以较弱的表示负担取得成功的希望，例如，即使表示专家策略本身要求过高，也只需要表示每个动作的预期价值。虽然以前的工作试图发展对基于价值的IL的理论理解（Abbeel和Ng，2004年；Syed和Schapire，2007年；Joshi等人，2026年；Moulin等人，2025a），现有方法要么不解决顺序设置，要么需要对环境进行结构假设；因此，我们对基于价值的 IL 的好处的理解仍然不完整。is used to assist policy learning (Garg et al., 2021; Swamy et al., 2021, 2022a), subsuming most inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) and apprenticeship learning methods (Abbeel and Ng, 2004; Syed and Schapire, 2007; Ho and Ermon, 2016). Rather than fitting the expert’s full distribution over actions, including potentially arbitrary or exogenous choices with equal value, value-based IL seeks to recover only enough information to match the expert’s return. This raises the hope that a value-based learner can succeed with a weaker representational burden, e.g., needing only to represent the expected value of each action even when representing the expert policy itself is too demanding. While previous works have sought to develop a theoretical understanding of value-based IL (Abbeel and Ng, 2004; Syed and Schapire, 2007; Joshi et al., 2026; Moulin et al., 2025a), existing methods either do not address the sequential setting or require structural assumptions on the environment; as a result, our understanding of the benefits of value-based IL remains incomplete.

在这项工作中，我们研究了价值函数和交互在 IL 中发挥作用的机制。我们的结果揭示了潜在的令人惊讶的相互作用：In this work, we investigate the mechanisms through which value functions and interaction help in IL. Our results reveal a potentially surprising interplay:互动放松了对学习者的表征要求。interaction relaxes representational demands on the learner.

利用同政策模仿，我们只需要一个能够实现专家价值函数的模型，绕过了实现专家政策本身的（通常更严格的）要求。此外，这在离线 IL 中是不可能的。Leveraging on-policy imitation, one only needs a model capable of realizing the expert’s value function, bypassing the (often stricter) requirement of realizing the expert’s policy itself. Furthermore, this is not possible in offline IL.我们的贡献详述如下。Our contributions are detailed below.

交互允许高效的基于价值的 IL（第 3 节）。Interaction permits efficient value-based IL (Section 3).我们引入 OVI（算法 1），这是第一个在统计和计算上具有预言机效率的算法，仅在We introduce OVI (Algorithm 1), the first algorithm that is statistically and computationally oracle-efficient under onlyQπ_E -realizability_——一种自然的表征假设，仅要求学习者能够表征专家的价值函数——-realizability_ —a natural representational assumption requiring only that the learner can represent the expert’s value function—前提是学习者可以交互式地询问专家的政策。provided that the learner can interactively query the expert on-policy.OVI 沿着学习者自己的轨迹询问专家，并在先前工作的基础上利用 IL 的鞍点公式来识别有用的价值函数（Moulin 等人，2025a；Swamy 等人，2021）。我们通过一项关于代表性优势的研究来补充这一点OVI queries the expert along the learner’s own trajectories and leverages a saddle-point formulation of IL to identify a useful value function, building on prior work (Moulin et al., 2025a; Swamy et al., 2021). We complement this with a study on the representational advantages ofQπ_E- 可实现性（第 3.2 节）：我们在几个自然问题类中表明，表示专家的价值函数比表示其策略要容易得多，而且 _reward 可实现性的假设较弱-realizability (Section 3.2): we show in several natural problem classes that representing the expert’s value function can be significantly easier than representing its policy, and furthermore that the weaker assumption of _reward realizability一般来说，对于（离线或交互式）IL 来说是不够的。is in general insufficient for (offline or interactive) IL.

基于交互式价值的 IL 根据经验可以使用更少的参数进行学习（第 3.3 节）。Interactive value-based IL empirically enables learning with fewer parameters (Section 3.3).我们在 Gymnasium 中凭经验验证了这些发现（Towers 等人，2025）；参见图 1。与我们的理论一致，OVI 的表现优于其他公司We validate these findings empirically in Gymnasium (Towers et al., 2025); see Figure 1. Consistent with our theory, OVI outperforms

基于交互式策略的方法（DAgger；Ross 等人，2011）和基于离线价值的方法（SPOIL；Moulin 等人，2025a），特别是当学习者的网络相对于专家的网络缩小时——在这种制度下，政策的可实现性可能会越来越受到侵犯。这些实验的代码可在 https://github.com/lviano/ovi 获取。both interactive policy-based methods (DAgger; Ross et al., 2011) and offline value-based methods (SPOIL; Moulin et al., 2025a), especially as the learner’s network shrinks relative to the expert’s—a regime in which policy realizability is likely to be increasingly violated. The code for these experiments is available at https://github.com/lviano/ovi.

对于基于价值的 IL 来说，交互是必要的（第 4 节）。Interaction is necessary for value-based IL (Section 4).我们证明，在没有条件强于We prove that without conditions stronger thanQ_π_E- 可实现性，仅给出专家轨迹的离线数据集的所有离线 IL 算法必然会随着环境中状态的数量或专家策略类的复杂性而扩展（定理 4.1）。我们进一步确定，即使在无限数据的限制下，基于价值的学习器的一个重要子类（包括流行的经验算法）也可能无法与离线环境中的专家策略竞争（定理 4.2）。直观上，之所以出现困难，是因为与传统的基于策略的 IL（例如，通过 BC 或 DAgger）不同，基于价值的学习者匹配专家的返回，而不匹配其状态访问分布（参见图 2）；我们的结果表明，这在交互作用下可以大大提高效率，但无法克服离线 IL 中存在的经过充分研究的分布转移挑战（Ross 等人，2011；Rajaraman 等人，2020；Foster 等人，2024）。- realizability, all offline IL algorithms given only an offline dataset of expert trajectories necessarily scale with the number of states in the environment or the complexity of the expert policy class (Theorem 4.1). We further establish that an important subclass of value-based learners (including popular empirical algorithms) can fail to compete with the expert policy in the offline setting even in the limit of infinite data (Theorem 4.2). Intuitively, the hardness arises because, unlike in traditional policy-based IL (e.g., via BC or DAgger), value-based learners match the expert’s return without matching its state visitation distribution (cf. Figure 2); our results indicate that this can be drastically more efficient under interaction but fails to overcome well-studied distribution shift challenges (Ross et al., 2011; Rajaraman et al., 2020; Foster et al., 2024) present in offline IL.

思想链学习中的计算表示权衡（第 5 节）。Computational-representational tradeoffs in chain-of-thought learning (Section 5).最后，我们通过证明 OVI 在专门用于思想链学习时可以比现有的基于奖励的方法（Joshi 等人，2026）实现指数级的计算改进，从而提供了关于 IL 中交互的好处的补充观点。思想链学习中基于价值的 IL 的先前理论方法从学习的结果奖励函数中提取模仿策略，但需要棘手的指数权重类型更新和响应级别采样。我们证明，利用交互和价值函数的可实现性，OVI 使用价值函数将完整响应的搜索分解为一系列易于处理的令牌级策略更新。这为成功使用该方法提供了新的理论理解和动力。Lastly, we provide a complementary perspective on the benefits of interaction in IL by showing that OVI can achieve an exponential computational improvement over existing reward-based approaches (Joshi et al., 2026) when specialized to chain-of-thought learning. Prior theoretical approaches for value-based IL in chain-of-thought learning extract an imitating policy from a learned outcome reward function, but require intractable exponential-weights-type updates and response-level sampling. We show that, leveraging interaction and value function realizability, OVI uses value functions to decompose the search over complete responses into a series of tractable token-level policy updates. This gives new theoretical understanding and motivation for the successful use of在政策on-policy更新（Agarwal 等人，2024 年；Gu 等人，2024 年；Yang 等人，2025 年）和updates (Agarwal et al., 2024; Gu et al., 2024; Yang et al., 2025) and过程奖励模型process reward models（Lightman 等人，2024；Uesato 等人，2023）在 LM 训练中。(Lightman et al., 2024; Uesato et al., 2023) in LM training.

交互何时对 IL 有帮助？When does interaction help in IL?超越了对改进视野依赖性的经典理解，我们的结果揭示了额外的（也许更强）Going beyond the classical understanding of improved horizon dependence, our results reveal additional (and perhaps stronger)代表性的representational互动的好处。总而言之，它们提供了基于价值的 IL 中交互和表示之间相互作用的全面描述。benefits of interaction. Taken together, they provide a comprehensive picture of the interplay between interaction and representation in value-based IL.

2 问题设置：基于价值的模仿学习2 Problem Setting: Value-Based Imitation Learning

离线和交互式模仿学习。Offline and interactive imitation learning.在我们考虑的模仿学习环境中，存在一个未知数In the imitation learning setting that we consider, there is an unknown专家政策 πexpert policy πE 和未知的 MDPE and an unknown MDPM，目标是学习一个性能同样好的策略, and the goal is to learn a policy that performs as well asπE，按预期回报衡量。形式上，为了容错E, as measured by expected return. 2.2 基于价值的模仿学习2.2 Value-Based Imitation Learning

我们的重点是Our focus is on基于价值的模仿学习value-based imitation learning，我们指的是从学习价值或奖励函数中导出策略的方法。古典, by which we mean methods that derive policies from learned value or reward functions. Classical基于政策的policy-based方法（例如 BC、DAgger）旨在模仿专家的完整条件动作分布methods (e.g., BC, DAgger) aim to imitate the full conditional action distribution of the expertπE 并因此匹配其性能。相比之下，E and to match its performance as a consequence. By contrast,基于价值的value-based学习者的目标是仅恢复足够的信息来匹配专家的返回，只要专家的轨迹分布（示例 3.1）或可利用的动态中的相关结构（示例 3.2）存在冗余，这可能是一个更简单的任务。learners aim to recover only enough information to match the expert’s return, which may be a simpler task whenever there is redundancy in the expert’s trajectory distribution (Example 3.1) or relevant structure in the dynamics that can be exploited (Example 3.2).

特别是，我们的目标是开发基于价值的 IL 算法，该算法在以下方面取得了成功In particular, our goal is to develop value-based IL algorithms that succeed with专家价值函数的唯一可实现性only realizability of the expert’s value function，基于价值的 IL 的自然代表性假设。用学习理论术语来说，学习者可以访问价值函数类, a natural representational assumption for value-based IL. Stated in learning-theoretic terms, the learner has access to a value function classQ ⊆{ ( Qh : X × A → [0 , Q最大]）max])H h =1_}_可以用来恢复良好的策略，并且_}_that can be used to recover a good policy, and我们对其表达性仅做出以下假设。we assume only the following on its expressivity.

假设2.1Assumption 2.1 ( Qπ_E-可实现性）-realizability)._类 Q 包含专家的价值函数，即 Q_The class Q contains the expert’s value function, i.e., Qπ_E εQ._∈Q._

相比之下，基于策略的方法，例如 BC 和 DAgger，通常需要策略可实现性假设（Foster 等人，2024），这使得它们在这种情况下不适用。在 3.2 节中，我们重点介绍了几种自然环境，其中By contrast, policy-based methods, such as BC and DAgger, commonly require policy realizability assumptions (Foster et al., 2024), which render them inapplicable in this setting. In Section 3.2, we highlight several natural settings in which代表专家的价值函数远不如实现专家的策略那么严格representing the expert’s value function is drastically less stringent than realizing the expert’s policy。因此，在第 3.1 节中，我们将开发交互式 IL 的算法，其统计率仅取决于值函数类的统计能力. Accordingly, in Section 3.1 we will develop algorithms for interactive IL whose statistical rates depend only on the statistical capacity of the value function classQ（例如，日志（(e.g., log(|问||Q|）对于有限函数类）而不是学习者使用的策略类。) for finite function classes) and not the policy class used by the learner.

将基于交互值的 IL 与相关设置进行比较。Comparing interactive value-based IL to related settings.最后，我们澄清了基于价值的 IL 设置与文献中考虑的相关 IL 设置之间的差异。下面提到的所有设置都允许学习者在 MDP 中展开，但使用与我们不同的专家交互协议。在Finally, we clarify the differences between our value-based IL setting and related IL settings considered in the literature. All of the settings mentioned below allow the learner to roll out in the MDP, but use protocols for interacting with the expert that differ from ours. In逆RLinverse RL，学习者与 MDP 或已知的 MDP 动态进行交互，但不会交互式地询问专家（Abbeel 和 Ng，2004；Syed 和 Schapire，2007；Ziebart 等，2008）。在, the learner interacts with the MDP or with known MDP dynamics, but does not query the expert interactively (Abbeel and Ng, 2004; Syed and Schapire, 2007; Ziebart et al., 2008). In价值反馈value-feedbackAggreVaTe（Ross and Bagnell，2014；Sun et al.，2017）等算法，学习者与 MDP 交互，可以观察专家值algorithms such as AggreVaTe (Ross and Bagnell, 2014; Sun et al., 2017), the learner interacts with the MDP and can observe the expert valueQπ_E ( x _h, a h），而鉴于学习者没有观察到奖励，目前尚不清楚如何在我们的设置中直接估计这些值。最后，在我们称之为的设置中), whereas it is unclear how to directly estimate these values in our setting given that the learner does not observe rewards. Lastly, in a setting we call强化学习与专家建议RL with expert advice，学习者既与 MDP 交互并交互式地查询专家（或很好地涵盖它的参考策略），又观察 MDP 中的奖励（Amortila 等人，2022；Tiapkin 等人，2024；Foster 等人，2025）。有关相关工作的全面讨论，请参阅附录 B。, the learner both interacts with the MDP and queries the expert (or a reference policy that covers it well) interactively, but additionally observes rewards in the MDP (Amortila et al., 2022; Tiapkin et al., 2024; Foster et al., 2025). 3 交互启用3 Interaction Enables QπE -基于可实现价值的模仿-Realizable Value-Based Imitation

本节的主要成果是 OVI，这是第一个可证明有效的基于价值的 IL 算法。The main result of this section is OVI, the first provably efficient algorithm for value-based IL underQπ_E-可实现性。在第 3.1 节中，我们描述了该算法及其保证。然后我们在 3.2 节中表明 _Q-realizability. In Section 3.1, we describe the algorithm and its guarantees. We then show in Section 3.2 that _Q_π_E- 可实现性可能弱于政策可实现性，而仅奖励可实现性是不够的。最后，在第 3.3 节中，我们提出了经验证据，表明在政策可实现性可能被违反的制度中，OVI 优于基于政策的方法（DAgger；Ross 等人，2011）和基于离线价值的方法（SPOIL；Moulin 等人，2025a）。-realizability can be weaker than policy realizability, while reward realizability alone is insufficient. Finally, in Section 3.3 we present empirical evidence that OVI outperforms both policy-based methods (DAgger; Ross et al., 2011) and offline value-based methods (SPOIL; Moulin et al., 2025a) in regimes where policy realizability is likely to be violated.

Whether a computationally efficient value-based IL algorithm can compute such a stationary policy remains open.

3.2 代表性优势3.2 Representational Advantages of QπE -可实现性-Realizability

OVI只需要访问实现专家价值函数的价值函数类，OVI requires only access to a value-function class that realizes the expert’s value function,Qπ_E。在本节中，我们表明，在许多自然环境中，_Q. In this section, we show that, in many natural settings, _Q_π_E-可实现性大大弱于先前基于政策和价值的方法所使用的假设。我们还确定，仅奖励可实现性是不够的。-realizability is substantially weaker than the assumptions used by prior policy- and value-based approaches. We also establish that reward realizability alone is insufficient.

3.2.1 价值的可实现性可能弱于政策的可实现性3.2.1 Value realizability can be weaker than policy realizability

经典的 IL 算法，例如 BC、DAgger 及其衍生算法，Classical IL algorithms, such as BC, DAgger, and their derivatives, are基于政策的policy-based：它们通过直接拟合专家的整个动作条件分布来进行操作，并且这些算法的性能通常依赖于: they operate by directly fitting the expert’s entire conditional distribution of actions, and the performance of these algorithms classically relies on a政策的可实现性policy realizability假设（Foster 等人，2024）。assumption (Foster et al., 2024).

假设3.1Assumption 3.1（政策可实现性）(Policy realizability). 政策类The policy class Π 包含专家策略，即 πcontains the expert policy, i.e., π E ∈ Π .

如果目标是重现专家的轨迹分布，那么这种假设是自然的This assumption is natural if the goal is to reproduce the expert’s trajectory distribution以无报酬的方式in a reward-free manner。但 IL 的目标较弱：匹配专家的回报。为了这个目标，尝试适应专家分布的具体细节可能是不必要和浪费的（例如，它如何打破同样良好的行动之间的联系，或者它如何响应与价值无关的特征）。下面的例子形式化了这种直觉。. But the objective in IL is weaker: to match the expert’s return. For this objective, it may be unnecessary and wasteful to attempt to fit the specific details of the expert distribution (e.g., how it breaks ties among equally good actions, or how it responds to features that are irrelevant for value). The examples below formalize this intuition.

乔希等人。 （2026）考虑我们环境的特殊情况，其中Joshi et al. (2026) consider the special case of our setting in whichH= 1，并表明基于价值的 IL 可能严格弱于基于策略的 IL（请注意，当= 1, and show that value-based IL can be strictly weaker than policy-based IL (note that whenH = 1, Q_π_E-可实现性降低为奖励可实现性）。特别是，他们-realizability reduces to reward realizability). 尽管这两个假设总体上不需要具有可比性，但我们预计在广泛的类似环境中，价值可实现性将弱于政策可实现性。我们在 3.3 节中的实验从经验上支持了这一点。Although the two assumptions need not be comparable in general, we expect value realizability to be weaker than policy realizability in a wide range of analogous settings. This is empirically supported by our experiments in Section 3.3.

直观上，导致定理 3.2 的困难根源在于，尽管奖励提供了有关哪些状态是有奖励的信息，但它没有提供有关哪些操作会导致这些状态的信息。因此，学习者无法使用奖励信息来学习如何一步步行动Intuitively, the source of hardness leading to Theorem 3.2 is that, even though the reward provides information on which states are rewarding, it does not provide information on which actions lead to those states. Consequently, the learner cannot use the reward information to learn how to act at steph= 1，并且必须求助于= 1, and must resort to要么either使用专家查询并克隆收到的操作using an expert query and cloning the received actionor在环境中应用学习者的动作并使用观察到的下一个状态来学习动态。总的来说，这需要分钟applying a learner’s action in the environment and using the observed next state to learn the dynamics. Overall this takes min{|X|,日志（log(|F| ) }/ε样品。相比之下，定理 3.1 表明这种依赖于转换的信息被编码为samples. By contrast, Theorem 3.1 shows that this transition-dependent information is encoded inQ_π_E并且可以被 OVI 利用。and can be exploited by OVI.

总而言之，All in all,我们认为we view QπE -可实现性作为有效学习的最小代表性假设-realizability as a minimal representational assumption for efficient learning：它比单独的奖励可实现性提供更多的信息，这是不够的，但比其他假设（例如，政策可实现性、: it is more informative than reward realizability alone, which is insufficient, but less demanding than other assumptions (e.g., policy realizability, realizability ofQπ对于无限多个策略for infinitely-many policiesπ，或奖励和转变的可实现性）在本文和之前的工作中讨论。接下来我们提供经验证据证明, or realizability of rewards and transitions) discussed in this paper and prior work. We next provide empirical evidence that the minimality ofQ_π_E-当使用表达能力有限的学习者时，可实现性转化为更有效的IL。-realizability translates to more effective IL when using learners with limited expressivity.

3.3 实验3.3 Experiments

我们测试当学习者能力有限时，第 3.2 节建议的代表性益处是否转化为实际表现。在四个 Gymnasium 环境中（Towers 等人，2025），专家是一个 RL 训练的策略，由一个网络表示，该网络具有两个隐藏层，每个隐藏层有 64 个神经元，而学习器使用相同的深度，但宽度为We test whether the representational benefits suggested by Section 3.2 translate into practical performance when the learner has limited capacity. In each of four Gymnasium environments (Towers et al., 2025), the expert is an RL-trained policy represented by a network with two hidden layers of 64 neurons each, while the learners use the same depth but have widths in{ 2 , 4 , 8 , 16 , 32 , 64 }。我们使用网络宽度作为表征能力的代理：随着学习者的网络相对于专家的网络变得更小，表征专家的动作分布应该变得越来越困难，并且基于策略的方法（例如 BC 和 DAgger）因此可能相对于 OVI 而言性能下降。这些实验的代码可在 https://github.com/lviano/ovi 获取。. We use network width as a proxy for representational capacity: as the learner’s network becomes smaller relative to that of the expert, representing the expert’s action distribution should become increasingly difficult, and policy-based methods such as BC and DAgger may therefore degrade relative to OVI. The code for these experiments is available at https://github.com/lviano/ovi.

我们在上述宽度范围内评估 OVI、SPOIL、BC 和 DAgger。我们使用 10 个专家轨迹用于离线方法，10 个学习者轨迹标记轮用于交互式方法，以及 50 个种子（更多实验细节请参阅附录 D）。图 1 显示，当学习器每层也有 64 个神经元时，BC 和 DAgger 在所有四种环境中都表现良好。然而，随着学习者网络的缩小，它们的性能会下降。相比之下，OVI 在所有学习器规模上都占主导地位，即使在最小宽度下也能获得良好的性能。 SPOIL 通常比 OVI 恶化得更快，尤其是在 CartPole-v1 和 Acrobot-v1 中，这与以下可能性一致：We evaluate OVI, SPOIL, BC, and DAgger across the above range of widths. We use 10 expert trajectories for the offline methods, 10 learner-trajectory labeling rounds for the interactive methods, and 50 seeds (see Appendix D for more experimental details). Figure 1 shows that when the learner also has 64 neurons per layer, BC and DAgger perform well in all four environments. Their performance degrades, however, as the learner network shrinks. In contrast, OVI dominates across all learner sizes and attains good performance even at the smallest width. SPOIL generally deteriorates faster than OVI, especially in CartPole-v1 and Acrobot-v1, consistent with the possibility thatQ_π_E-在这些实验中，可实现性比SPOIL所要求的封闭型可实现性条件更容易满足。在这些实验中，我们使用 OVI 的平稳近似；伪代码请参见算法 2。-realizability is easier to satisfy in these experiments than the closure-type realizability conditions required by SPOIL. We use a stationary approximation to OVI in these experiments; see Algorithm 2 for pseudocode.

最后，我们在合成中比较了 DAgger 和 OVIFinally, we compare DAgger and OVI in a syntheticQ_π_E-realized MDP 强调指出，尽管两种 IL 算法都是交互式的，但 OVI 实现了与 DAgger 不同的模仿机制。-realizable MDP to highlight that, despite both IL algorithms being interactive, OVI implements a different imitation mechanism than DAgger.

VI 策略类包含广泛的算法所使用的策略，这些算法通过将激活函数（例如，softmax、ReLU、贪婪）应用于值函数的线性组合来从值函数中提取策略The VI policy class encompasses the policies used by a broad class of algorithms that extract policies from value functions by applying activation functions (e.g., softmax, ReLU, greedy) to linear combinations of value functions fromQ .9特别是，它包括先前基于理论和经验值的 IL 方法（Moulin 等人，2025a；Joshi 等人，2026；Garg 等人，2021；Watson 等人，2023）以及 OVI 使用的隐式策略类别。详细信息请参见附录 G。以下下界表明，在In particular, it includes the implicit policy classes used by prior theoretical and empirical value-based IL methods (Moulin et al., 2025a; Joshi et al., 2026; Garg et al., 2021; Watson et al., 2023) as well as by OVI. 定理 4.2 的直觉是，价值引发的策略必须在具有同等高价值的行动之间均匀随机化，即使专家本身可能只发挥这些行动的子集。在定理 4.2 的构造中（如图 6 所示），这意味着学习器的输出策略以恒定概率脱离分布，从而导致所有数据集大小的错误率恒定。我们预计，在 VI 政策类别的更广泛定义下，不可识别性继续存在，该政策类别根据行动分配概率The intuition for Theorem 4.2 is that the value-induced policies must randomize uniformly among actions with equally high value, even though the expert itself may only play a subset of those actions. In the construction of Theorem 4.2 (illustrated in Figure 6), this implies that the learner’s output policy goes out of distribution with constant probability, thus incurring a constant error rate for all dataset sizes. We expect that unidentifiability continues to hold under broader definitions of the VI policy class which assign probabilities to actions based on their价值概况value profiles（孙等人，2019a）。(Sun et al., 2019a).

我们在本节中的下界（定理 4.1 和定理 4.2）表明，一般来说，交互对于只学习Our lower bounds in this section (Theorem 4.1 and Theorem 4.2) show that interaction is necessary in general for learning with onlyQ_π_E-可实现性。我们通过简要讨论如何在某些设置中放宽交互和表征要求来结束本节。-realizability. We conclude this section by briefly discussing how the interaction and representational requirements can be relaxed in certain settings.

覆盖范围绕过离线下限。Coverage circumvents the offline lower bound.如果专家在具有足够覆盖范围的状态空间上引入分布，则分布转移将不再导致输出 VI 策略（例如 SPOIL）的离线算法出现问题（附录 I）。特别是，在定理 I.1 中，我们证明了 SPOIL 下的样本复杂性保证If the expert induces a distribution over the state space with sufficient coverage, then distribution shift no longer causes issues for offline algorithms that output a VI policy such as SPOIL (Appendix I). In particular, in Theorem I.1, we prove sample complexity guarantees for SPOIL underQ_π_E- 可实现性、专家最优性和标准覆盖条件（Munos，2003；Antos 等，2008；Chen 和 Jiang，2019；Xie 等，2023；Amortila 等，2024c；Jiang 和 Xie，2025）。然而，我们认为这个假设在实践中并不容易满足。-realizability, expert optimality and a standard coverage condition (Munos, 2003; Antos et al., 2008; Chen and Jiang, 2019; Xie et al., 2023; Amortila et al., 2024c; Jiang and Xie, 2025). However, we do not consider this an easy assumption to satisfy in practice.

5 思想链学习：计算表征权衡5 Chain-of-Thought Learning: Computational-Representational Tradeoffs

前面的部分表明，交互放松了学习者的表征要求。在这里，我们展示了如何在 LM 推理的设置中实例化 OVI，以实现与现有方法相比计算效率的指数级提高（Joshi 等人，2026），前提是专家的价值可以实现，从而为 IL 中交互的价值提供了补充视角。The preceding sections showed that interaction relaxes the representational requirements for the learner. Here, we show how OVI can be instantiated in the setting of LM reasoning to achieve exponential improvements in computational efficiency over existing approaches (Joshi et al., 2026), provided that the expert’s value can be realized, thereby providing a complementary perspective on the value of interaction in IL.

数学和代码生成等推理任务已成为现代 LM 研究的中心焦点（Wei 等，2022；Li 等，2022）。在这些领域中，通常可以使用客观评估器（例如编码的单元和集成测试以及数学的形式验证器），并且可以用作“基本事实”奖励函数。为了解决这些问题，LM 被训练来生成一系列标记，称为Reasoning tasks such as mathematics and code generation have become a central focus of modern LM research (Wei et al., 2022; Li et al., 2022). In these domains, objective evaluators (such as unit and integration tests for coding and formal verifiers for math) are often available and can be used as a “ground truth” reward function. To solve these problems, LMs are trained to generate a sequence of tokens, called a思想链chain of thought(CoT)，然后输出最终答案。为了获得 CoT 推理，LM 可以通过 IL 进行训练，通常是通过下一个标记预测（Ouyang et al., 2022; Cobbe et al., 2021）对由提示、专家完整的思维链和正确的最终答案组成的专家推理轨迹进行训练。(CoT), before outputting the final answer. To acquire CoT reasoning, LMs can be trained via IL, commonly via next-token prediction (Ouyang et al., 2022; Cobbe et al., 2021) on expert reasoning traces consisting of a prompt, the expert’s complete chain-of-thought, and the correct final answer.

CoT 学习（Malach，2024；Joshi 等人，2025）已正式化如下。 LM 被建模为一种自回归策略，在给出提示的情况下CoT learning (Malach, 2024; Joshi et al., 2025) has been formalized as follows. The LM is modeled as an autoregressive policy that, given a promptx ∈X，对响应进行采样, samples a responsey ∈Y通过viay ∼ π ( · | x），其中), wherey=（= (y 1 , . . . , y H− 1 , y H) 是一个序列) is a sequence ofH令牌，属于由 Σ 表示的令牌词汇表，自回归生成为tokens, belonging to a token vocabulary denoted by Σ, generated autoregressively asy h∼πh ∼ π ( · | x , y 1 , . . . , y h− 1), h = 1 , . 。 。 , H, . . . , H，以及最后一个标记, and the last tokeny H是最终的答案。这个交互定义了一个is the final answer. This interaction defines a代币级 MDPtoken-level MDP与地平线with horizonH，其中状态是迄今为止的部分代，, where states are the partial generations so far,x h=（= (x , y 1: h− 1) ε×∈X × Σh−_1，动作是下一个标记, the action is the next tokeny_h ε_h ∈Σ，下一个状态是Σ, and the next state isx h +1 = ( x , y 1: h）。学习者有权访问数据集). The learner is given access to a datasetD = { ( x , y ) }，其中提示, where the promptx从给定的提示分布中采样，响应由专家生成，is sampled from a given prompt distribution, and the response is generated by the expert,y ∼ π E( · | x）。奖励函数是一个二元函数). The reward function is a binary结果验证者outcome verifier定义为defined asr : X × Y →{ 0 , 1 }以便政策的预期回报，so that the expected return of a policy,Jπ=E= Eπ [ r ( x , y)]，相当于它的答案准确率。 IL 的目标随后转化为制定政策的问题)], is equivalent to its answer accuracy. The goal of IL then translates to the problem of producing a policyπ谁的最终答案的准确性可以与专家的相媲美。out whose final-answer accuracy competes with the expert’s.

基于非交互式奖励的 IL 在计算上很棘手。Non-interactive reward-based IL is intractable computationally.利用动态的确定性和已知性，对 CoT 学习的自然简化是将 CoT 问题视为A natural simplification to CoT learning, exploiting that the dynamics are deterministic and known, is to treat the CoT problem as anH= 1 个问题（即上下文强盗），其中提示= 1 problem (i.e., a contextual bandit), where the promptx是上下文和响应is the context and the responsey ∈ ΣH是一个动作。在奖励（或结果验证者）可实现性假设（假设 3.2）下，Joshi 等人。 (2026) 估计奖励 �is an action. (2026) estimate a reward �r从示威活动中from the demonstrationsD使用指数权重类型过程，在每次迭代时，必须求解 arg maxusing an exponential-weights-type procedure which, at each iteration, must solve arg maxy∈ Σ 小时H r �( x , y) 提取给定提示的响应) to extract a response for a given promptx .11他们的程序存在两个计算障碍。首先，奖励优化是在整个响应空间上进行的Their procedure presents two computational barriers. First, the reward optimization is over the full response spaceY = ΣH，对于长视野来说，这在计算上是困难的。其次，对奖励类别执行指数权重更新是不可行的，因为它需要枚举奖励类别。, which is computationally intractable for long horizons. Second, performing exponential weight updates over the reward class is infeasible as it requires enumerating over the reward class.

基于价值的交互式 IL 的计算优势。Computational benefits of value-based interactive IL.为了利用 CoT 学习固有的顺序性质，我们可以将 OVI 直接应用于由提示分布、LM 和结果验证器交互生成的 token 级 MDPTo exploit the inherently sequential nature of CoT learning, we can apply OVI directly to the token-level MDP generated by the interaction of the prompt distribution, the LM, and the outcome verifierr。每一步. At each steph，OVI 学习一个值函数，该函数自然地导出 softmax 令牌采样分布（算法 1 的第 7 行）。这分解了之前在响应空间 Σ 上棘手的搜索, OVI learns a value function that naturally induces a softmax token sampling distribution (Line 7 of Algorithm 1). This decomposes the previously intractable search over the response space ΣH进入intoH本地搜索步骤。此外，问题的每个阶段都是由 OVI 使用简单的线性最大化预言来解决的，用于价值函数搜索（算法 1 的第 6 行）和策略的 softmax 更新（算法 1 的第 7 行），这可以大规模实施。事实上，我们的方法提出了价值网络的损失函数，可以通过反向传播结合现代优化器将其最小化。local search steps. Moreover, each stage of the problem is solved by OVI using simple linear maximization oracles for the value function search (Line 6 of Algorithm 1) and softmax updates for the policy (Line 7 of Algorithm 1), which can be implemented at scale. Indeed, our approach suggests a loss function for the value network which can be minimized via backpropagation coupled with modern optimizers.

值得注意的是，OVI 的计算优势优于 Joshi 等人的序列级方法。 （2026）的代价是更强的代表性要求（实现专家每一步的价值函数，而不是简单地获得最终答案的奖励）以及对专家的交互式查询访问。这两个附加假设已被广泛证明可以提高性能；第一个自然可以被解释为需要一个能够表达过程验证器（或过程奖励模型；Lightman et al., 2024；Uesato et al., 2023）的架构，而不是简单的结果验证器，第二个对应于在策略蒸馏的设置（Agarwal et al., 2024；GuIt is worth noting that the computational benefits of OVI over sequence-level methods like Joshi et al. (2026) come at the cost of a stronger representational requirement (realizing the expert’s value function for each step rather than simply the reward of the final answer) as well as interactive query access to the expert. Both of these additional assumptions have been widely shown to improve performance; the first can naturally be interpreted as requiring an architecture capable of expressing a process verifier (or process reward model; Lightman et al., 2024; Uesato et al., 2023), as opposed to simply an outcome verifier, and the second corresponds to the setting of on-policy distillation (Agarwal et al., 2024; Gu

11我们注意到，基于奖励的下界定理 3.2 不适用于 CoT 学习，因为代币级 MDP 的动态是已知的。11We note that our reward-based lower bound Theorem 3.2 does not apply in CoT learning since the dynamics in the token-level MDP are known.

等，2024； Yang et al., 2025）已广泛应用于语言建模。因此，我们的结果为在 LM 训练中使用过程奖励模型和策略交互提供了新颖的理论依据。这些成果是否转化为语言模型的基于实用价值的 IL 算法是一个非常有趣的经验问题。et al., 2024; Yang et al., 2025) which has been widely employed in language modeling. Our results therefore provide a novel theoretical justification for the use of process reward models and on-policy interaction in LM training. Whether these gains translate to practical value-based IL algorithms for language models is a very interesting empirical question.

6 结论6 Conclusion

IL 中交互的价值是什么？在这项工作中，我们研究了交互和价值函数估计在 IL 中发挥作用的机制，揭示了一种可能令人惊讶的相互作用：交互放松了对学习者的表征要求，使其能够在仅实现专家的价值函数的同时取得成功，而不是专家的完整策略或所有学习者生成的策略的价值函数。我们通过 OVI 形式化了这一点，OVI 是一种基于交互式值的 IL 算法，在统计和计算方面具有高效性What is the value of interaction in IL? In this work, we investigated the mechanisms through which interaction and value function estimation help in IL, revealing a perhaps surprising interplay: interaction relaxes the representational demands on the learner, allowing it to succeed while realizing only the expert’s value function, as opposed to the expert’s full policy or the value functions of all learner-generated policies. We formalized this through OVI, an interactive value-based IL algorithm that is statistically and computationally efficient underQ_π_E-单独的可实现性，以及一个互补的下限，确定在这个最小假设下相互作用对于 IL 是必要的。专门针对思想链推理，我们进一步表明，这些表征优势可以转化为相对于非交互式方法的指数计算收益（Joshi 等人，2026）。这些发现表明，IL 中相互作用的好处远远超出了对改善水平依赖性的经典理解。-realizability alone, and a complementary lower bound establishing that interaction is necessary for IL under this minimal assumption. Specializing to chain-of-thought reasoning, we further showed that these representational benefits can translate into exponential computational gains over non-interactive approaches (Joshi et al., 2026). These findings suggest that the benefits of interaction in IL extend well beyond the classical understanding of improved horizon dependence.

一些有趣的未来方向仍然存在。在技术方面，首先获得一个有价值的Several interesting future directions remain open. On the technical side, it would first be valuable to obtain anO ( ε−_2) 非凸类的比率 _Q) rate for nonconvex classes _Q使用计算效率高的算法。其次，在下with a computationally efficient algorithm. Second, while underπ电子实现几乎无地平线界限是可能的（Foster 等人，2024），对于基于价值的 IL 是否可以实现同样的目标是一个有趣的悬而未决的问题。最后，在价值函数指定错误的情况下研究基于价值的 IL 会很有趣；我们预计相关的错误指定形式与基于价值的强化学习中的错误指定形式不同（Chen 和 Jiang，2019；Du 等人，2020；Amortila 等人，2023、2024a；Maran 和 Szepesvári，2026）。在实证方面，了解 OVI（或更广泛的基于价值的 IL 方法）是否可以在语言模型蒸馏中有效是很有价值的，因为专家策略的可实现性不太可能成立。E-realizability nearly horizon-free bounds are possible (Foster et al., 2024), whether the same is achievable for value-based IL is an interesting open question. Finally, it would be interesting to study value-based IL in the presence of value function misspecification; we expect that the relevant forms of misspecification differ from their counterparts in value-based RL (Chen and Jiang, 2019; Du et al., 2020; Amortila et al., 2023, 2024a; Maran and Szepesvári, 2026). On the empirical side, it would be valuable to understand whether OVI (or value-based IL methods more broadly) can be effective in language model distillation, where expert policy realizability is unlikely to hold.

B 附加相关工作B Additional Related Work

我们概述了密切相关的工作。在附录 B.1 中，我们讨论了可用于基于价值的 IL 方法的理论结果，在附录 B.2 中，我们介绍了注重经验性能的基于价值的 IL 算法。我们广泛使用“基于价值的 IL”，包括奖励学习、逆强化学习、学徒学习、矩匹配和基于 Q 函数的方法，因为所有方法都使用学习到的奖励/价值信息来导出策略，而不是直接拟合专家行动分布。We provide an overview of closely related work. In Appendix B.1, we discuss theoretical results available for valuebased IL methods, and in Appendix B.2 we cover value-based IL algorithms that focus on empirical performance. We use “value-based IL” broadly to include reward-learning, inverse reinforcement learning, apprenticeship learning, moment matching, and Q-function-based methods, since all use learned reward/value information to derive a policy rather than directly fitting the expert action distribution.

B.1 模仿学习的理论保证B.1 Theoretical Guarantees for Imitation Learning

正如正文中提到的，我们的工作旨在当专家政策可实现性不需要成立时建立专家样本复杂性界限。虽然我们是第一个研究这个问题的人As mentioned in the main text, our work aims to establish expert-sample complexity bounds when expert policy realizability need not hold. While we are the first to study this problem underQ_π_E-仅考虑可实现性，之前的工作已经检验了我们下面讨论的几个替代假设。-realizability alone, prior work has examined several alternative assumptions that we discuss below.

在 Pomerleau（1988，1991）的开创性工作中提出行为克隆之后，平行的工作线正式化After behavior cloning was proposed in the seminal work of Pomerleau (1988, 1991), a parallel line of work formalized逆向强化学习inverse reinforcement learning（IRL；Russell，1998；Ng 和 Russell，2000），其目标是推断出解释专家行为的奖励函数，通常是专家最佳的众多奖励之一。密切相关的工作(IRL; Russell, 1998; Ng and Russell, 2000), whose goal is to infer a reward function that explains the expert’s behavior, typically among many rewards for which the expert is optimal. Closely related work on学徒学习apprenticeship learning（Abbeel 和 Ng，2004；Abbeel 等，2008；Syed 和 Schapire，2007；Syed 等，2008；Ziebart 等，2008）使用这种基于奖励的视角来学习与专家表现相匹配的策略。(Abbeel and Ng, 2004; Abbeel et al., 2008; Syed and Schapire, 2007; Syed et al., 2008; Ziebart et al., 2008) uses this reward-based perspective to learn a policy that matches the expert’s performance.

Ross 和 Bagnell (2014) 引入了 AggreVaTe，这是一种交互式无悔缩减方法，可以通过价值信息增强专家行动查询。也就是说，当学习者访问状态-动作对时（Ross and Bagnell (2014) introduced AggreVaTe, an interactive no-regret reduction that augments expert action queries with value information. That is, when the learner visits a state-action pair (x, 一个x, a ), Qπ_E( _x, 一个( _x, a）被观察到。这个神谕被称为) is observed. This oracle is referred to as价值反馈value feedback并且比我们的设置更强，在我们的设置中，学习者可以查询专家的操作，但不会观察奖励或专家的值。他们的框架还产生了与基于神经网络的函数逼近兼容的实用算法；参见孙等人。 （2017）；程等人。 （2018）。众所周知，基于价值的反馈以及对交互式专家的访问可以在错误传播中实现更好的水平依赖性（Ross 和 Bagnell，2014 年；Sun 等人，2017 年），这可以为基于策略的 IL 带来代表性收益（Foster 等人，2024 年）。and is stronger than our setting, where the learner can query expert actions but does not observe rewards or expert values. Their framework also yields practical algorithms compatible with neural-networkbased function approximation; see Sun et al. (2017); Cheng et al. (2018). Value-based feedback, together with access to an interactive expert, is known to allow for better horizon dependence in error propagation (Ross and Bagnell, 2014; Sun et al., 2017), which can lead to representational benefits in policy-based IL (Foster et al., 2024).

与 OVI 最接近的概念前身是 Swamy 等人的矩匹配框架。 (2021)，特别是他们的“On-Q”模板和相关的 DAeQuIL 算法。该框架还包括奖励和“Off-Q”时刻匹配模板，并通过以下方式分析模仿差距The closest conceptual predecessor to OVI is the moment-matching framework of Swamy et al. (2021), especially their “On-Q” template and the associated DAeQuIL algorithm. This framework also includes reward and “Off-Q” momentmatching templates, and analyzes imitation gaps through力矩恢复能力 µmoment recoverability µ：将行动价值时刻与精度相匹配: matching action-value moments to precisionε产生次优的阶数界限yields a suboptimality bound of orderµHε，在经典的基础上进行改进, improving on the classicalH2BC 式分析中的误差传播因子。根据经验，斯瓦米等人。 (2021) 表明 DAeQuIL 在森林导航任务中可以优于 BC 和 DAgger，他们将其归因于动作值矩匹配引起的模式搜索行为。然而，尽管交互式算法具有更好的错误传播特性，Foster 等人。 （2024）表明，没有一种交互式算法可以在所有可能的策略类别中统一改进离线算法。因此，误差传播方式分析似乎不足以捕捉交互的好处。我们的贡献是互补的：我们用计算预言机高效的算法实例化“On-Q”视角，提供样本复杂性分析error-propagation factor in BC-style analyses. Empirically, Swamy et al. (2021) show that DAeQuIL can outperform BC and DAgger in a forestnavigation task, which they attribute to the mode-seeking behavior induced by action-value moment matching. However, despite the better error-propagation properties of interactive algorithms, Foster et al. (2024) showed that no interactive algorithm can uniformly improve upon an offline one across all possible policy classes. Therefore, the error-propagationstyle analysis does not seem sufficient to capture the benefits of interaction. Our contribution is complementary: we instantiate the “On-Q” perspective with a computationally oracle-efficient algorithm, provide a sample-complexity analysis underQ_π_E-可实现性，并表明仅靠这种代表性假设在离线环境中是不够的。-realizability, and show that this representational assumption alone is insufficient in the offline setting.

接下来，我们进一步回顾基于价值的IL，区分（i）在专家演示的固定数据集上运行的离线算法与（ii）可以额外访问环境并收集轨迹而无需观察奖励的变体。然后，我们讨论主要的基于政策的 IL 方法，然后是基于价值的 IL 的有影响力的实证研究。Next, we review value-based IL further, distinguishing (i) offline algorithms that operate on a fixed dataset of expert demonstrations from (ii) variants that can additionally access the environment and collect trajectories without observing rewards. Then, we discuss the main policy-based IL methods, followed by influential empirical work on value-based IL.

通过在线 MDP 访问或已知动态进行基于价值的模仿学习。Value-based imitation learning with online MDP access or known dynamics.区分三种密切相关的访问模型很有用。首先，经典学徒学习和 IRL 方法（Abbeel 和 Ng，2004 年；Syed 和 Schapire，2007 年）假设了解包含真正未知奖励的线性奖励类，以及对 MDP 动态的完美了解。在具有未知动态的表格问题中，Syed 和 Schapire (2007) 仅在专家经常访问的状态-动作对上估计转换内核，并将所有其他转换发送到悲观的死状态。他们表明，这种受限模型足以保留保证，但代价是样本复杂度界限稍差。同样在表格设置中，Rajaraman 等人。 (2020) 用二次水平依赖性来表征最坏情况下的离线模仿率It is useful to distinguish three closely related access models. First, classical apprenticeship-learning and IRL methods (Abbeel and Ng, 2004; Syed and Schapire, 2007) assumed knowledge of a linear reward class containing the true unknown reward, as well as perfect knowledge of the MDP dynamics. In tabular problems with unknown dynamics, Syed and Schapire (2007) instead estimated the transition kernel only on state-action pairs frequently visited by the expert and sent all other transitions to a pessimistic dead state. They showed that this restricted model suffices to retain a guarantee, at the cost of a slightly worse sample-complexity bound. Also in the tabular setting, Rajaraman et al. (2020) characterize worst-case offline imitation rates with quadratic horizon dependenceH2并表明，对于确定性专家来说，使用转换模型可以将水平依赖性提高至少一个and show that, for deterministic experts, access to the transition model can improve the horizon dependence by at least a√H因素。拉贾拉曼等人。 （2021b）后来证明了该设置下的匹配下界。factor. Rajaraman et al. (2021b) later proved a matching lower bound in that setting.

第二行工作假设对 MDP 的无奖励在线访问：学习者可以在环境中推出策略，但它不会观察奖励，并且通常通过预先收集的演示来获取专家信息，专家A second line of work assumes reward-free online access to the MDP: the learner may roll out policies in the environment, but it does not observe the reward and typically obtains expert information through precollected demonstrations, expert

23

附录目录Appendix table of contents

占用率、专家状态或功能，而不是通过价值反馈。徐等人。 (2023) 删除了上面在表格对抗 IL 中讨论的已知转换假设，并提出了一种通过将专家演示与无奖励环境交互相结合来实现极小极大最优专家样本复杂性的方法。沙尼等人。 （2022）提出了一种基于奖励和政策序列之间交替更新的方法。维亚诺等人。 (2022) 在对学习者策略的强烈探索性假设下，为具有线性、未知奖励和动态的 MDP 中的无限水平 IL 开发极小极大/近点方法，该方法后来被 Viano 等人删除。 （2024）。穆林等人。 (2025b)进一步开发无限范围线性MDP的乐观探索工具并将其应用于IL。在状态或特征匹配变体中，此类方法有时可以使用专家状态或特征而不是专家动作，将它们与仅观察的模仿联系起来（Sun et al., 2019b; Torabi et al., 2018, 2019; Kidambi et al., 2021; Viel et al., 2025）。除了线性函数近似之外，Xu 等人。 （2024）将保证扩展到需要通用函数近似来近似奖励和环境动态的设置。最后，最近的工作还研究了实例相关和二阶保证（即，随着专家和学习者价值函数的方差而缩放的界限）：Wu 等人。 (2025) 在分数匹配框架中给出一阶和二阶界限，而 Li 等人。 (2026b) 证明了基于模型的对抗性 IL 的近乎最优的二阶保证。occupancies, or expert states or features rather than through value feedback. Xu et al. (2023) removed the knowntransition assumption discussed above in tabular adversarial IL and proposed a method achieving the minimax optimal expert-sample complexity by combining expert demonstrations with reward-free environment interaction. Shani et al. (2022) presented an approach based on alternating updates between sequences of rewards and policies. Viano et al. (2022) develop minimax/proximal-point methods for infinite-horizon IL in MDPs with linear, unknown rewards and dynamics under a strong exploratory assumption on the learner’s policies, which was later removed by Viano et al. (2024). Moulin et al. (2025b) further develop optimistic exploration tools for infinite-horizon linear MDPs and apply them to IL. In state- or feature-matching variants, such methods can sometimes use expert states or features rather than expert actions, connecting them to imitation from observation alone (Sun et al., 2019b; Torabi et al., 2018, 2019; Kidambi et al., 2021; Viel et al., 2025). Beyond linear function approximation, Xu et al. (2024) extended the guarantees to the setting in which general function approximation is required to approximate the reward and the dynamics of the environment. Finally, recent work also studies instance-dependent and second-order guarantees (i.e., bounds that scale with the variance of the expert and learner value functions): Wu et al. (2025) give first- and second-order bounds in a score-matching framework, while Li et al. (2026b) prove near-optimal second-order guarantees for model-based adversarial IL.

最后，另一条相关但不同的线路除了专家信息之外还为学习者提供在线奖励观察，使问题更接近于有专家建议的强化学习，而不是无奖励的IL。例如，蒂普金等人。 (2024) 研究了从最优专家收集的状态-动作对的离线记录可用于初始化强化学习中的学习器策略的设置。他们表明，在表格和线性 MDP 中，这可以比没有专家数据的 RL 实现更好的样本复杂性。在代表性方面，Amortila 等人。 (2022) 通过“一步”重置、访问交互式最优专家以及实现最优状态值函数的类来研究强化学习。他们提供了一种计算效率高的算法来近似最优策略，表明专家建议可以使原本难以实现价值实现的设置变得易于处理。这与仅在价值函数可实现性下的规划或强化学习形成对比，其中查询或计算障碍在相关设置中是已知的（Weisz 等人，2021；Liu 等人，2023）。Finally, another related but distinct line gives the learner online reward observations in addition to expert information, making the problem closer to RL with expert advice than to reward-free IL. For example, Tiapkin et al. (2024) studied the setting in which an offline record of state-action pairs collected from an optimal expert can be used to initialize the learner policy in RL. They show that in tabular and linear MDPs, this can achieve better sample complexity than RL without expert data. On the representation side, Amortila et al. (2022) study RL with “one-step” resets, access to an interactive optimal expert, and a class that realizes the optimal state-value function. They provide a computationally efficient algorithm to approximate the optimal policy, showing that expert advice can make otherwise difficult value-realizable settings tractable. This contrasts with planning or RL under value-function realizability alone, where query or computational barriers are known in related settings (Weisz et al., 2021; Liu et al., 2023).

这些工作是对我们的设置的补充：它们利用奖励观察、已知动态、无奖励环境交互，无需学习者状态专家操作查询或 RL 专家建议，而 OVI 仅使用无奖励 MDP 推出以及对学习者访问状态的专家操作查询。These works are complementary to our setting: they exploit reward observations, known dynamics, reward-free environment interaction without learner-state expert action queries, or expert advice for RL, whereas OVI uses only reward-free MDP rollouts together with expert action queries on learner-visited states.

使用记录的专家数据进行基于价值的模仿学习。Value-based imitation learning with logged expert data.与我们的设置最接近的离线对应物是 Moulin 等人。 （2025a）。他们的算法 SPOIL 表明，通过强加闭包类型的值可实现性假设，可以在离线 IL 中避免专家策略可实现性：类The closest offline counterpart to our setting is Moulin et al. (2025a). Their algorithm, SPOIL, shows that expert-policy realizability can be avoided in offline IL by imposing a closure-type value realizability assumption: the classQ必须意识到must realizeQπ对于每项政策for every policyπ在隐式策略类 Π 中in the implicit policy class ΠQ该算法可以优化或输出。这个假设与只需要that the algorithm may optimize over or output. This assumption is qualitatively different from, and substantially stronger than, requiring onlyQπ_E εQ_，因为它要求价值类在许多学习者生成的策略下封闭，而不仅仅是包含专家的价值函数。这种区别对于解释我们的离线下限很重要：下限表明∈Q_ , since it asks the value class to be closed under many learner-generated policies rather than only to contain the expert’s value function. This distinction is important for interpreting our offline lower bound: the lower bound shows thatQ_π_E- 一般情况下，对于基于离线价值的 IL 而言，仅可实现性是不够的，而 SPOIL 的离线保证依赖于我们主要设置中不存在的额外封闭结构。-realizability alone is insufficient for offline value-based IL in general, while the offline guarantees of SPOIL rely on additional closure structure that is absent from our main setting.

Joshi 等人研究了不同的记录数据设置。 (2026)，他们将正确演示的学习建模为上下文强盗中的离线 IL，相当于具有 Horizo​​n 的 MDPA different logged-data setting was studied by Joshi et al. (2026), who model learning from correct demonstrations as offline IL in a contextual bandit, equivalently an MDP with horizonH= 1. 他们假设正确性或奖励模型属于有限类，而不是假设专家策略的可实现性，并且他们仅通过离线演示来设计统计上有效的算法。此外，当实例化时，他们改进了 Syed 和 Schapire (2007) 的算法= 1. They assume that the correctness, or reward, model belongs to a finite class, rather than assuming expert-policy realizability, and they design statistically efficient algorithms from offline demonstrations alone. Moreover, they improve the algorithm of Syed and Schapire (2007) when instantiated withH= 1，从而在专家最优时获得更快的速率。因为= 1, thereby obtaining faster rates when the expert is optimal. BecauseH= 1，没有顺序分布变化，也没有需要学习或利用的重要过渡动态。= 1, there is no sequential distribution shift and no nontrivial transition dynamics to learn or exploit.

最近基于价值的 IL 方法还研究了价值结构如何减轻复合误差。徐等人。 （2026）表明，IQ-Learn 式的目标可以简化为行为克隆，但仍然会遭受复合错误，并且他们引入了贝尔曼约束作为将价值信息从演示状态传播到演示未涵盖的状态的机制。在我们的设置中，这个想法的一个变体可以用来过滤给定的可实现类A recent value-based IL method also investigates how value structure can mitigate compounding errors. Xu et al. (2026) show that IQ-Learn-style objectives can reduce to behavior cloning and still suffer compounding errors, and they introduce Bellman constraints as a mechanism for propagating value information from demonstrated states to states not covered by the demonstrations. In our setting, a variant of this idea could be used to filter a given realizable classQ通过删除“时间”变化大于 1 的函数；这保证降低了复杂性by removing functions with “temporal” variations greater than one; this is guaranteed to reduce the complexity ofQ同时仍然提供可实现性while still providing realizability ofQ_π_E。实际上，这可以通过在 OVI 目标中添加一个正则化项来实现，该项会惩罚较大的时间变化。. In practice, this could be implemented by adding a regularization term to the OVI objective that penalizes large temporal variations.

基于策略的模仿学习。Policy-based imitation learning.从历史上看，解决IL的最常见范例是最大化在给定政策类别中观察到的专家行动的可能性。在离线形式中，BC 通过对专家轨迹的监督学习来拟合专家行动分布，早期的 BC 式系统包括 ALVINN（Pomerleau，1988，1991）。Historically, the most common paradigm for tackling IL is to maximize the likelihood of the observed expert actions over a given policy class. In its offline form, BC fits the expert action distribution by supervised learning on expert trajectories, with early BC-style systems including ALVINN (Pomerleau, 1988, 1991).

24

附录目录Appendix table of contents

DAgger 等交互式变体（在实践中非常流行）和相关的无悔缩减，而是收集学习者访问过的状态的专家标签，从而解决当离线克隆策略部署在其自己的轨迹分布上时出现的协变量变化（Ross 和 Bagnell，2010；Ross 等人，2011）。理论保证通常用包含专家策略的策略类 Π 的复杂性来表示Interactive variants such as DAgger—very popular in practice—and related no-regret reductions instead collect expert labels on states visited by the learner, thereby addressing the covariate shift that appears when an offline cloned policy is deployed on its own trajectory distribution (Ross and Bagnell, 2010; Ross et al., 2011). Theoretical guarantees are typically expressed in terms of the complexity of a policy class Π that contains the expert policyπE（例如，参见 Rajaraman 等人，2021a；Swamy 等人，2022b）。 Li和Zhang（2022）更广泛地研究了这种基于分类的在线IL观点，包括不可实现的设置。最近，福斯特等人。 （2024）通过表明在适当的政策可实现性和对数损失可学习性假设下，离线BC可以具有比标准复合误差分析所建议的更好的水平依赖性，从而完善了经典图景。E (see, for example, Rajaraman et al., 2021a; Swamy et al., 2022b). Li and Zhang (2022) study this classification-based online IL viewpoint more generally, including nonrealizable settings. More recently, Foster et al. (2024) refined the classical picture by showing that, under suitable policy realizability and log-loss learnability assumptions, offline BC can have much better horizon dependence than suggested by the standard compounding-error analysis.

后续工作调查当学习者的策略类无法完全实现专家策略时会发生什么。罗哈吉等人。 (2025) 分析了政策错误指定下的自回归学习和 IL，表明错误放大在错误指定的设置中再次出现，并且下一个代币或 BC 式目标面临计算统计权衡。他们还提出了稳健的 BC 变体，其保证量化了对范围和错误指定的依赖。埃斯皮诺萨-戴斯等人。 (2025) 介绍了 GUITAR，这是一种在错误指定下的高效交互式 IL 算法，它使用从专家占用度量中采样的状态初始化的结构完整性条件和环境访问，并通过错误指定的优势类概念而不是统计分歧来控制保证。Follow-up work investigates what happens when the expert policy is not exactly realizable by the learner’s policy class. Rohatgi et al. (2025) analyze autoregressive learning and IL under policy misspecification, showing that error amplification reappears in misspecified settings and that next-token or BC-style objectives face computational-statistical tradeoffs. They also propose robustified BC variants whose guarantees quantify the dependence on horizon and misspecification. Espinosa-Dice et al. (2025) introduce GUITAR, an efficient interactive IL algorithm under misspecification that uses a structural completeness condition and environment access initialized from states sampled from the expert occupancy measure, with guarantees controlled by an advantage-class notion of misspecification rather than by statistical divergences.

最近，斯里拉曼等人。 （2026）研究了专家样本可能被破坏的环境：有一定的可能性，向学习者显示的动作是从不同的策略而不是专家的策略中得出的。他们表明，任何基于策略的离线 IL 算法都会遭受指数样本复杂性的影响，而他们的交互式算法 NAIL 通过额外查询专家来避免这种指数爆炸。Most recently, Sriraman et al. (2026) studied a setting in which expert samples may be corrupted: with some probability, the action shown to the learner is drawn from a different policy rather than the expert’s. They show that any offline policy-based IL algorithm suffers exponential sample complexity, whereas their interactive algorithm, NAIL, avoids this exponential blow-up by additionally querying the expert.

为了总结我们对 IL 理论文献的回顾，我们在表 3 中报告了我们的结果与获得的界限以及其他算法所需的代表性条件的比较。To conclude our review of the theoretical literature in IL, we report a comparison of our results with the bounds obtained and the representational conditions required by other algorithms in Table 3.

B.2 基于经验价值的模仿学习B.2 Empirical Value-Based Imitation Learning

尽管早期经验IL的成功包括行为克隆系统，如ALVINN（Pomerleau，1988，1991），但基于经验价值的IL很大程度上源于IRL和学徒学习。早期的奖励学习方法包括最大利润规划和最大熵或相对熵 IRL（Ratliff 等人，2006 年；Ziebart 等人，2008 年；Boularias 等人，2011 年）。这些方法学习解释专家行为的奖励或成本函数，然后通过在学习的目标下进行规划或策略优化来得出策略。深度变体，例如引导成本学习和最大熵深度 IRL，用神经成本或奖励函数取代线性奖励（Finn 等人，2016 年；Wulfmeier 等人，2015 年）。然而，这些方法通常继承了 Ziebart 等人的“RL-in-the-loop”结构。 （2008），需要在不断变化的学习奖励下重复规划或策略优化步骤，这在高维环境中可能是昂贵的。Although early empirical IL successes include behavior-cloning systems such as ALVINN (Pomerleau, 1988, 1991), empirical value-based IL largely grew out of IRL and apprenticeship learning. Early reward-learning methods include maximum-margin planning and maximum-entropy or relative-entropy IRL (Ratliff et al., 2006; Ziebart et al., 2008; Boularias et al., 2011). These methods learn a reward or cost function that explains the expert’s behavior and then derive a policy by planning or policy optimization under the learned objective. Deep variants, such as guided cost learning and maximum-entropy deep IRL, replace linear rewards with neural cost or reward functions (Finn et al., 2016; Wulfmeier et al., 2015). However, these methods typically inherit the “RL-in-the-loop” structure of Ziebart et al. (2008), requiring repeated planning or policy-optimization steps under a changing learned reward, which can be expensive in high-dimensional environments.

其他方法用对抗性或占用匹配目标取代明确的规划。何等人。 (2016) 使用策略梯度优化开发了无模型学徒学习方法，而 Ho 和 Ermon (2016) 则引入了生成对抗性 IL (GAIL)，它在训练判别器/成本信号和更新策略之间交替，并在最初的实现中使用信任区域策略优化 (TRPO; Schulman et al., 2015)。 GAIL 和相关的对抗性 IL 方法激发了学习奖励、判别器-演员-批评者方法、离策略分布匹配和平稳分布校正等后续工作（Fu 等人，2018；Kostrikov 等人，2019、2020；Nachum 等人，2019）。这些方法可能是有效的，但对抗性 IL 通常需要大量样本，并且可能难以稳定，因为策略优化器必须不断适应非平稳的学习奖励或判别器。Other methods replace explicit planning with adversarial or occupancy-matching objectives. Ho et al. (2016) developed model-free apprenticeship-learning methods using policy-gradient optimization, while Ho and Ermon (2016) introduced generative adversarial IL (GAIL), which alternates between training a discriminator/cost signal and updating the policy, using Trust Region Policy Optimization (TRPO; Schulman et al., 2015) in the original implementation. GAIL and related adversarial IL methods inspired follow-up work on learned rewards, discriminator-actor-critic methods, off-policy distribution matching, and stationary-distribution correction (Fu et al., 2018; Kostrikov et al., 2019, 2020; Nachum et al., 2019). These methods can be effective, but adversarial IL is often sample-hungry and can be difficult to stabilize because the policy optimizer must continually adapt to a nonstationary learned reward or discriminator.

最近的非对抗性基于价值和 Q 的方法旨在避免这种不稳定性。例如，SQIL 将演示转换为稀疏奖励 RL 问题（Reddy 等人，2019），而 Garg 等人的 IQ-Learn 方法。 （2021）学习单软More recent non-adversarial value- and Q-based methods aim to avoid some of this instability. For example, SQIL converts demonstrations into a sparse-reward RL problem (Reddy et al., 2019), while the IQ-Learn method of Garg et al. (2021) learns a single softQ-隐式代表奖励和策略的函数。此类方法与我们下限中考虑的广泛的基于值的 IL 算法密切相关：它们通过将 softmax、贪婪或相关激活应用于学习的值函数来提取策略。 IQ-Learn 已在模拟机器人任务中进行了评估（Garg 等人，2021）；相关的 Q 学习、IRL 和矩匹配思想也已适用于自回归序列建模和 LM 蒸馏，包括 SequenceMatch（Cundy 和 Ermon，2024）、用于语言模仿的可扩展逆软 Q 学习（Wulfmeier 等人，2024）和对抗性矩匹配蒸馏（Jia，2024）。基于演示的奖励学习和 IRL 风格的方法也已应用于 LM 监督微调（SFT）和推理，无需偏好标签或验证器（Li 等人，2024；Cai 等人，2026）。我们-function that implicitly represents both the reward and the policy. Such methods are closely aligned with the broad class of value-based IL algorithms considered in our lower bound: they extract a policy by applying a softmax, greedy, or related activation to a learned value function. IQ-Learn has been evaluated in simulated robotics tasks (Garg et al., 2021); related Q-learning, IRL, and moment-matching ideas have also been adapted to autoregressive sequence modeling and LM distillation, including SequenceMatch (Cundy and Ermon, 2024), scalable inverse soft-Q learning for language imitation (Wulfmeier et al., 2024), and adversarial moment-matching distillation (Jia, 2024). Demonstration-based reward-learning and IRL-style approaches have also been applied to LM supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reasoning without preference labels or verifiers (Li et al., 2024; Cai et al., 2026). 用于语言模型微调的基于策略的模仿学习。Policy-based imitation learning for language model fine-tuning.基于策略的、类似 DAgger 的同策略蒸馏方法也被应用于 LM 微调。阿加瓦尔等人。 (2024) 使用教师反馈根据学生自己生成的输出来训练学生模型，Lu 和 Thinking Machines Lab (2025) 明确将策略蒸馏定义为对学生访问的状态查询或评估教师。这些方法提供了本文研究的交互式协议的基于策略的模拟。他们强大的经验表现推动了在蒸馏中更广泛地使用交互，但我们的结果提出了一个补充假设：当学生的表达能力远不如老师时，基于交互价值的蒸馏可能特别有用，因为它可以针对价值等价行为，而不是教师的完整行动分布。图 1 中的 Gym 实验为此提供了初步证据。在 LM 蒸馏中测试相同的机制仍然是一个重要的经验方向。Policy-based, DAgger-like on-policy distillation methods have also been applied to LM fine-tuning. Agarwal et al. (2024) train student models on their own generated outputs using teacher feedback, and Lu and Thinking Machines Lab (2025) explicitly frames on-policy distillation as querying or evaluating a teacher on states visited by the student. These methods provide a policy-based analogue of the interactive protocols studied in this paper. Their strong empirical performance motivates the broader use of interaction in distillation, but our results suggest a complementary hypothesis: when the student is much less expressive than the teacher, interactive value-based distillation may be especially useful because it can target value-equivalent behavior rather than the teacher’s full action distribution. The Gym experiments in Figure 1 provide preliminary evidence for this. Testing the same mechanism in LM distillation remains an important empirical direction.

C.1.2 专家最优性对于放松条件下的学习是必要的C.1.2 Expert optimality is necessary for learning under relaxed QπE -可实现性-realizability

For stochastic suboptimal experts, this can increase the probability of actions rarely played by the expert.

C.2 QπE -可实现性可能明显弱于-Realizability Can Be Strictly Weaker Than π 外源噪声下的电子可实现性E-Realizability under Exogenous Noise

在本节中，我们给出一个例子，其中专家策略的可实现性比In this section, we give an example in which expert-policy realizability is more demanding thanQ_π_E-可实现性。该示例是外生块 MDP (ExBMDP)，这是一种标准的丰富观测模型，其中内生控制问题是低维的，但基于观测的专家策略必须解码高维观测。一个典型的例子是具有高维相机观测的机器人，其中包含（小）内生状态和独立于机器人动作而演化的背景（例如电视）。我们将下面的设置形式化，并在随后的小节中陈述和证明结果。-realizability. The example is an exogenous block MDP (ExBMDP), a standard rich-observation model in which the endogenous control problem is low-dimensional, but the observation-based expert policy must decode a high-dimensional observation. A typical example is a robot with high-dimensional camera observations that contain the (small) endogenous state and a background that evolves independently of the robot’s actions (e.g., a television). We formalize the setting below, and the results are stated and proved in subsequent subsections.

此示例的一个限制是，始终选择操作 0 的单个策略在该系列中的每个 MDP 中都是最优的，因此该构造涉及精确的表示复杂性而不是控制难度。我们认为这是可以接受的，因为构建的目的是表明精确的专家策略可实现性可能比One limitation of this example is that a single policy that always chooses action 0 is optimal in every MDP in the family, so the construction concerns exact representational complexity rather than control hardness. We view this as acceptable because the purpose of the construction is to show that exact expert-policy realizability can be much more demanding thanQ_π_E-可实现性。-realizability.

动机与样本复杂性保证中出现的复杂性项直接相关。 BC 和 DAgger 等基于策略的方法旨在模仿专家的行动分布。在专家策略可实现性假设下，它们的保证通常取决于策略类别 Π 的复杂性，满足The motivation is directly tied to the complexity terms that appear in sample-complexity guarantees. Policy-based methods such as BC and DAgger aim to imitate the expert’s action distribution. Under an expert-policy realizability assumption, their guarantees typically depend on the complexity of a policy class Π satisfyingπ E ∈Π，通过诸如 log 之类的术语Π, through a term such as log| Π |（或日志覆盖数）。相反，我们基于价值的保证取决于价值函数类的复杂性(or a log-covering number). In contrast, our value-based guarantees depend on the complexity of a value-function classQ满意的satisfyingQπ_E εQ_ ，通过诸如 log 之类的术语∈Q_ , through a term such as log|问||Q| .

下面的结构表明这两个量可以呈指数级差异。我们构建了 ExBMDP 系列The construction below shows that these two quantities can be exponentially different. We construct a family of ExBMDPs{MB{Mb : b ∈Bn}和确定性、次优专家and deterministic, suboptimal experts{πb : b ∈Bn}由平衡布尔解码器索引indexed by balanced Boolean decodersb : { 0 , 1 }n →{ 0 , 1 }, 其中, wheren是第 1 阶段观察空间中的位数。任何包含家族中所有专家的与专家无关的策略类都必须具有指数级大的度量熵：对于每个is the number of bits in the observation space at stage 1. Any expert-agnostic policy class that contains all experts in the family must have exponentially large metric entropy: for everyε εε ∈ (0 , 1 / 2),

哪里wheredΠ 是总变异度量。相比之下，二维类Π is the sup-total variation metric. By contrast, a two-dimensional classQ实现专家动作价值函数realizes the expert action-value functionQπ Mb b对于每一个for everyb ∈Bn, 和, and

非正式地，构造如下。我们考虑一个内生状态是单个位的例子Informally, the construction is as follows. We consider an instance where the endogenous state is a single bits ∈ {s ∈{ 0 , 1 }，而专家的内生政策只是发挥作用, and the expert’s endogenous policy simply plays actiona = s行动之中among the actionsA = { 0 , 1 , 2 }。然而，学习者并没有观察到. However, the learner does not observes直接。相反，学习者观察一个高维二进制字符串directly. Instead, the learner observes a high-dimensional binary stringw ∈ {w ∈{ 0 , 1 }n，并恢复, and recoverings来自fromw需要计算解码器requires computing a decoderb ( w）。因此，精确模仿专家的基于观察的策略必须计算). Thus, an observation-based policy that exactly imitates the expert must computeπ E , 1( w）=) =b ( w），并且实现专家的策略类的复杂性必须随着解码器的复杂性而扩展。另一方面，), and the complexity of a policy class realizing the expert must scale with the complexity of the decoder. On the other hand, theQ- 功能不需要这个解码器。在第一层，动作 0 和 1 都是安全且值等价的，而动作 2 是不安全的。因此-function does not need this decoder. At the first layer, actions 0 and 1 are both safe and value-equivalent, while action 2 is unsafe. HenceQπ_E只需要知道与值相关的区别only needs to know the value-relevant distinction of{ 0 , 1 }与vs.{ 2 }。硬解码器仅确定专家在两个安全操作之间的平局决胜，这就是造成这种分离的原因。每位专家获得回报1. The hard decoder determines only the expert’s tie-breaking between the two safe actions, which is what creates this separation. Every expert obtains return 1/_ 2，而总是选择动作 0 的策略获得最优回报 1。因此，即使每个专家都严格次优，分离仍然成立。/_ 2, while the policy that always chooses action 0 obtains the optimal return 1. Thus the separation holds even though every expert is strictly suboptimal.

C.2.4 策略的可实现性需要指数级大的类别C.2.4 Policy realizability requires an exponentially large class

我们证明，任何包含家族中所有专家的策略类 Π 都必须在超总变差度量下具有指数级大的覆盖数We show that any policy class Π that contains all experts in the family must have an exponentially large covering number under the sup-total-variation metricdΠ。特别是，如果 Π 是有限的，那么它必须具有指数级大的基数。这表明政策可实现性假设比价值函数可实现性假设强得多，因为相同的二维类Π. In particular, if Π is finite, then it must have exponentially large cardinality. This shows that a policy realizability assumption is much stronger than a value-function realizability assumption, since the same two-dimensional classQ认识专家realizes the expertQ-适用于家族中每个专家的功能，而实现所有专家的任何单个策略类都必须具有指数复杂性。-function for every expert in the family, while any single policy class realizing all experts must have exponential complexity.

C.2.5 分离定理C.2.5 Separation theorem

现在我们总结一下这次分离。结果比较了输入基于策略和基于价值的保证的复杂性术语。它表明，在硬解码器系列中，（i）任何与专家无关的类在所有解码器上统一满足专家策略可实现性We now summarize the separation. The result compares the complexity terms that enter policy-based and value-based guarantees. It shows that on the hard-decoder family, (i) any expert-agnostic class satisfying expert-policy realizability uniformly over all decodersb具有指数度量熵，而（ii）基于价值的保证has exponential metric entropy, while (ii) a value-based guarantee underQ_π_E-可实现性取决于实现专家动作值函数的二维类的复杂性。-realizability depends on the complexity of a two-dimensional class realizing the expert action-value function.

为什么策略需要解码器但Why the policy needs the decoder but the Q - 功能没有。-function does not.在第 1 层，对于任何观察At layer 1, for any observationw，专家的行动是, the expert’s action isπ 1b(w）=) =b(w).因此精确的行为克隆必须恢复解码器).Thus exact behavioral cloning must recover the decoderb.不过，专家.However, the expertQ- 功能在-function at the同样的观察是same observation isQπ Mb b, 1(w,_0) =_Qπ Mb b, 1(w,_1) = 1/2、和2, and_Qπ Mb b, 1(w,_2) = 0，独立于w,_2) = 0, which is independent of_b(w). 的).The解码器决定专家选择哪个安全动作，但这两个安全动作具有相同的值。这就是为什么更容易代表专家的观点decoder determines which safe action the expert chooses, but both safe actions have the same value. This is why it is easier to represent the expert’sQ-功能而不是代表专家的政策。-function than to represent the expert’s policy.

这里，专家严格来说是次优的，因为它在第二层选择了动作 1 而不是动作 0。这不会影响第一层的决胜机制：动作0和1具有相同的专家Here the expert is strictly suboptimal because it chooses action 1 rather than action 0 at the second layer. This does not affect the tie-breaking mechanism at the first layer: actions 0 and 1 have the same expertQ-value，并且恢复准确的专家策略仍然需要依赖于解码器的规则-value, and recovering the exact expert policy still requires the decoder-dependent rulew �→ b ( w）。因此一个简单的). Thus a simpleQ-功能可以对应于大量的专家策略，即使这些专家不是最优的。在此结构中使专家的第一层动作唯一最优将迫使-function can correspond to an enormous set of expert policies even when those experts are suboptimal. Making the expert’s first-layer action uniquely optimal in this construction would forceQπb对解码器进行编码，消除当前与解码器无关的见证类。to encode the decoder, eliminating the present decoder-independent witness class.

为什么外源结构很重要。Why the exogenous structure matters.如果内源状态If the endogenous states直接观察，专家将是恒定复杂性策略were observed directly, the expert would be the constant-complexity policyπ ¯1b(s）=) =s_and_π¯ 2b(y) = 1 在大小为 7 的内生状态空间上。受控动力学) = 1 on an endogenous state space of size 7.The controlled dynamics在内生坐标中奖励也很简单。and rewards are also simple in endogenous coordinates.

之所以出现复杂性，只是因为观察图将内生位隐藏在外生变异中，The complexity appears only because the observation map hides the endogenous bit inside exogenous variation,w = ηsb(ξ)ε{_0∈{_0,1}n_.从原始观察中恢复内生位需要计算}n_.Recovering the endogenous bit from the raw observation requires computings=b(w). 因此).Thus the基于观察的专家政策因素为observation-based expert policy factors asπ 1b(w）=) =π¯ 1b(φ⋆ 1 ,b(w)) =b(w).因此，专家政策的可实现性需要).Expert-policy realizability therefore requires代表硬解码器。相比之下，representing the hard decoder. By contrast,Qπb在解码器之间是相同的。这就是为什么同样的二维is identical across decoders. This is why the same two-dimensionalQ-class 对所有解码器统一工作-class works uniformly for all decodersb，而任何实现所有相应专家的策略类都必须呈指数级大。, whereas any policy class that realizes all corresponding experts must be exponentially large.

D 实验细节D Experiment Details

本节包括额外的实验结果和正文中介绍的实验的详细信息。首先，我们在用于下限的实例中进行一些实验，以展示基于离线价值的 IL 的失败This section includes additional experimental results and details on the experiments presented in the main text. To start with, we include some experiments in the instance we use for our lower bound to showcase the failure of offline value-based IL underQ_π_E-可实现性且无需额外假设。作为输出 VI 策略的代表性离线算法，我们使用 SPOIL (Moulin et al., 2025a)。这些结果在附录 D.1 中描述。接下来，在附录 D.2 中，我们提供了有关体育馆实验的更多详细信息和评论。-realizability and no additional assumptions. As a representative offline algorithm that outputs a VI policy, we use SPOIL (Moulin et al., 2025a). These results are described in Appendix D.1. Next, in Appendix D.2 we provide additional details and comments about the Gymnasium experiments.

D.1 下界实例的实验D.1 Experiments on the Lower Bound Instance

我们首先研究基于离线 VI 策略的算法（例如 SPOIL）的性能。我们在定理 4.2 中的否定结果预测至少在一种环境中会出现持续的次优状态。为了在实践中观察这一预测，我们重复实验 500 次，每次都让对手在两个环境之间进行统一选择We start by investigating the performance of an offline VI-policies-based algorithm, such as SPOIL. Our negative result in Theorem 4.2 predicts a constant suboptimality in at least one environment. To observe this prediction in practice, we repeat the experiment 500 times, each time letting the adversary choose uniformly between the two environmentsM1 和1 andM2 在定理 4.2 的证明中使用的 Hard 系列。我们在图 3 中报告了它们的平均次优性，这是最坏情况次优性的下限。事实上，至少有一个2 in the hard family used in the proof of Theorem 4.2. We report their average suboptimality in Figure 3, which is a lower bound on the worst-case suboptimality. Indeed, at least one ofM1 和1 andM2 的次优性不小于平均值。如图 3 所示，OVI 达到零次优值，而 SPOIL 仍保持在我们的构造预测的恒定次优值。2 has suboptimality no smaller than the average. As Figure 3 shows, OVI reaches zero suboptimality, whereas SPOIL remains at the constant suboptimality predicted by our construction.

D.2 神经网络的实现D.2 Implementation with Neural Networks

对于体育馆实验，我们考虑一个固定的For the Gymnasium experiments, we consider a stationary

算法 1 的近似值。在每个 epoch 的开始，我们用上一个 epoch 的学习器策略输出收集的一个轨迹进行标记，approximation of Algorithm 1. At the beginning of each epoch, we label one trajectory collected with the learner policy output by the previous epoch,πℓ，而不是从, rather than fromdπ h出out正如我们的有限视野理论所规定的那样。我们用专家的行动来标记这样的轨迹。此时，我们计算 OVI 使用的目标的经验近似值，并通过以下方式计算近似鞍点：as our theory for finite horizon would prescribe. We label such trajectories with expert actions. At this point, we compute the empirical approximation of the objective used by OVI and compute an approximate saddle point byK在线学习算法的迭代。算法 2 中给出了生成的伪代码。对于实验，我们设置iterations of the online learning algorithms. The resulting pseudocode is given in Algorithm 2. For the experiments, we setK= 50，而我们沿学习者轨迹改变最大查询数量，如图 4 所示。策略的指数权重更新通过 Soft DQN（Haarnoja 等人，2017）进行近似，如 Moulin 等人所述。 （2025a）。此外，我们观察到，当通过聚合专家和学习者轨迹形成状态数据集时，OVI 的性能会有所提高。这种数据混合在同策略蒸馏中很常见（Agarwal et al., 2024），我们证明它扩展了交互的代表性优势；参见附录 J。= 50 while we vary the maximum number of queries along the learner trajectories as shown in Figure 4. The exponential weights update for the policy is approximated via Soft DQN (Haarnoja et al., 2017) as in Moulin et al. (2025a). Moreover, we have observed that OVI’s performance improves when the state dataset is formed by aggregating both expert and learner trajectories. Such data mixing is common in on-policy distillation (Agarwal et al., 2024) and we prove that it extends the representational benefits of interaction; see Appendix J.

在像 CartPole-v1 和 Acrobot-v1 这样最简单的环境中，我们发现离线收集（对于 SPOIL 和 BC）或在线收集然后由专家标记（对于 OVI 和 DAgger）的 10 个轨迹通常足以匹配专家的表现。图4中，在LunarLander-v2和Pendulum-v1等更复杂的环境中，我们增加了专家样本的数量，但我们认为没有必要增加In the easiest environments like CartPole-v1 and Acrobot-v1, we find that 10 trajectories collected offline (for SPOIL and BC) or online and then labeled by the expert (for OVI and DAgger) usually suffice to match the expert performance. In Figure 4, in more complicated environments such as LunarLander-v2 and Pendulum-v1, we increased the number of expert samples, but we did not find it necessary to increase the value ofK .

作为基线，我们使用 DAgger（Ross 等人，2011），这是一种需要策略可实现性的交互式方法。我们还使用标准 BC（Pomerleau，1991），一种需要专家策略可实现性的离线方法，以及 SPOIL（Moulin 等人，2025a），一种不需要交互式专家访问但假设更强的离线方法。As baselines, we use DAgger (Ross et al., 2011), an interactive method requiring policy realizability. We also use standard BC (Pomerleau, 1991), an offline method requiring expert-policy realizability, and SPOIL (Moulin et al., 2025a), an offline method that does not require interactive expert access but assumes the strongerQΠQ- 实现条件，即-realizability condition, namely,Qπ ∈Q对于每一个for everyπ επ ∈ Π Q .

D.3 健身房实验的其他细节和评论D.3 Additional Details and Comments on the Gym Experiments

我们在一些 Gymnasium 环境中运行了上述算法，并在接下来报告结果。特别是，我们首先通过 Pendulum-v1 中的 DQN（Mnih 等人，2015）和 CartPole-v1 和 Acrobot-v1 中的 PPO（Schulman 等人，2017）训练一个具有 2 个隐藏层（每个隐藏层有 64 个神经元）的专家网络，然后考虑以下两种设置。首先，我们考虑通过完全相同的架构（2 个隐藏层，64 个神经元）参数化的学习器。在这种情况下，专家可实现性显然成立。因此，BC 和 DAgger 预计能够很好地工作。其次，我们考虑较小的We ran the above algorithms in some Gymnasium environments and report the results next. In particular, we first trained an expert network with 2 hidden layers with 64 neurons each via DQN (Mnih et al., 2015) in Pendulum-v1 and PPO (Schulman et al., 2017) in CartPole-v1 and Acrobot-v1 and then considered the following two settings. First, we considered a learner parameterized via exactly the same architecture (2 hidden layers with 64 neurons). In this case, expert realizability clearly holds. Therefore, BC and DAgger are expected to work well. Second, we considered smaller

学习器网络（2 个隐藏层，每层有 16、8、4 或 2 个神经元）。在第二种情况下，专家可实现性是一个缺乏动机的假设，因此我们预计，随着专家与学习者规模差距的增加，SPOIL 和 OVI 等方法会更加有效。learner networks (2 hidden layers with 16, 8, 4, or 2 neurons per layer). Expert realizability is a poorly motivated assumption in this second case, and we therefore expected methods like SPOIL and OVI to be more efficient as the expert–learner size gap increases.

上述假设确实被我们在 CartPole-v1 和 Acrobot-v1 中的实验所证实。当专家可实现性成立时（图 4 的顶行），我们看到所有四种方法本质上是等效的，并且最终都设法匹配专家策略的性能。相比之下，当学习器网络变得更小时，我们看到避免专家可实现性假设的方法取得了更好的性能。此外，对于我们测试的最大尺寸差距（图 4 的最后一行），交​​互的好处变得显而易见，其中 OVI 优于 SPOIL。因此，这表明这里考虑的网络参数化不足以代表The aforementioned hypotheses are indeed confirmed by our experiments in CartPole-v1 and Acrobot-v1. When expert realizability holds (top row of Figure 4), we see that all four methods are essentially equivalent and all eventually manage to match the performance of the expert policy. By contrast, when the learner network becomes smaller, we see that the methods that avoid the expert-realizability assumption achieve better performance. Moreover, the benefits of interaction become evident for the largest size gap we tested (in the last row of Figure 4) in which OVI outperforms SPOIL. This suggests, therefore, that the network parametrization considered here is not powerful enough to represent theQ-算法生成的策略序列的函数。一个有趣的额外观察是，在 CartPole-v1 和 Acrobot-v1 中，基于价值的方法明显优于基于策略的方法。相比之下，在 Pendulum-v1 和 LunarLander-v2 中，交互式方法（即 OVI 和 DAgger）是最好的。因此，对于某些环境，交互似乎是性能的关键，而在其他环境中，基于价值的表示更为重要。我们认为这样的-functions of the policy sequence generated by the algorithm. An interesting additional observation is that in CartPole-v1 and Acrobot-v1, value-based methods clearly outperform policy-based ones. In contrast, in Pendulum-v1 and LunarLander-v2, the interactive methods, i.e., OVI and DAgger, are the best ones. Therefore, for some environments it seems that interaction is key for performance, while in others value-based representation is more important. We think that such依赖于实例instance-dependent现象提出了有趣的开放性问题。phenomena raise interesting open questions.

Pendulum-v1 环境自然会伴随连续动作，但我们通过动作离散化使其适应我们的设置，这是一种在实践中使用的技术（Dadashi 等人，2022），最近由 Cao 和 Xie（2026）进行了分析。值得注意的是，在这种情况下，我们注意到 OVI 的表现优于标准 SPOIL，这可能是因为 OVI 对代表权的要求较弱。The Pendulum-v1 environment naturally comes with continuous actions, but we adapted it to our setting via action discretization, a technique used in practice (Dadashi et al., 2022) and recently analyzed by Cao and Xie (2026). Notably, in this case, we notice that OVI outperforms the standard SPOIL, potentially because OVI imposes a weaker requirement on the representational power of theQ- 类。专家们获得了以下平均回报：CartPole-v1 中为 500，-class. The experts achieved the following average returns: 500 in CartPole-v1,− 77 .Acrobot-v1 中为 1，LunarLander-v2 中为 270，1 in Acrobot-v1, 270 in LunarLander-v2 and− 176 .Pendulum-v1 中为 6。上的零点6 in Pendulum-v1. The zero point on they-axis 设置为通过非常次优的策略实现的以下回报：-axis is set to the following returns achieved by very suboptimal policies:−Acrobot-v1 中为 500 个，CartPole-v1 中为 8 个，500 in Acrobot-v1, 8 in CartPole-v1,− 1650 .Pendulum-v1 中的 0 和0 in Pendulum-v1 and−LunarLander-v2 中为 600。对于每个环境，我们使用从 0 到 49 的不同随机种子运行每个 IL 算法 50 次。按照体育馆环境中的常见做法（Garg 等人，2021），我们对离线方法的专家轨迹进行二次采样，并仅以固定频率保留交互式方法沿学习者轨迹的专家操作标签。对于样本统计，每个保留的标签对于离线方法算作一个专家样本，对于交互式方法算作一个专家查询。因此，一条长度为600 in LunarLander-v2. For each environment, we ran each IL algorithm 50 times with different random seeds from 0 to 49. As is common practice in Gymnasium environments (Garg et al., 2021), we subsampled the expert trajectories for offline methods and retained expert-action labels along learner trajectories only at a fixed frequency for interactive methods. For sample accounting, each retained label counts as one expert sample for offline methods and one expert query for interactive methods. Thus, a trajectory of lengthT贡献contributes⌈T/f⌉⌈T/f ⌉以子采样频率进行采样或查询samples or queries at subsampling frequencyf .

对于所有算法，我们调整了它们的超参数以优化性能，没有专家与学习者之间的差距，然后为较小学习器网络规模的实验保留相同的超参数。超参数的详细列表位于我们的代码随附的自述文件中。对于 SPOIL 和 OVI，我们通过 Soft DQN 学习来近似策略更新For all algorithms, we tuned their hyperparameters to optimize the performance with no expert–learner gap and then kept the same hyperparameters for the experiments with smaller learner network sizes. The detailed list of hyperparameters is in the README file accompanying our code. For SPOIL and OVI, we approximate the policy update via Soft DQN learning over theQ-价值，不是来自奖励，而是来自我们的理论分析所激发的损失。实验可以在 CPU 上运行。我们在个人笔记本电脑上运行它们。但请注意，运行所有实验可能需要几个小时。-values, not from rewards, but with the loss motivated by our theoretical analysis. The experiments can be run on CPUs. We ran them on a personal laptop. Note, however, that running all the experiments can take several hours.
