我们使用以下版本的性能差异引理（Howard，1960；Kakade 和 Langford，2002）：它将两种策略之间的性能差距表示为一种策略在另一种策略占据下的优势之和。We use the following version of the performance difference lemma (Howard, 1960; Kakade and Langford, 2002): it expresses the performance gap between two policies as a sum of advantages of one policy under the occupancy of the other.引理E.9Lemma E.9（性能差异引理）(Performance difference lemma). 设 π 和 πLet π and π′ 制定任意政策。然后，be arbitrary policies. 第一部分Part I

主要结果证明Proofs of Main Results

在本节中，我们包括正文中省略的证明。附录E提供了集中和优化的技术结果，将用于证明附录F中算法1的样本复杂性保证，并证明附录G中的下限。In this section, we include the proofs omitted from the main text. Appendix E provides technical results in concentration and optimization that will be used to prove the sample complexity guarantees of Algorithm 1 in Appendix F, and to prove the lower bound in Appendix G.

接下来，我们使用引理 F.1 推导 OVI 的样本复杂度保证。在高层次上，证明直觉是注意到引理 F.1 将次优差距的控制减少到两项：政策制定者的平均遗憾和经验鞍点目标的平均估计误差。因此，只要保证 RNext, we use Lemma F.1 to derive a sample complexity guarantee for OVI. At a high level, the proof intuition is to notice that Lemma F.1 reduces the control of the suboptimality gap to two terms: the average regret of the policy player and the average estimation error of the empirical saddle-point objective. Thus, it suffices to ensure that Rπ h(x) 呈亚线性增长)growssublinearlyinK_foreachstage_h_andstate_x∈X，这是通过无悔来实现的,whichisachievedbytheno-regret算法 1 中的更新。估计误差项由均匀浓度参数控制update in Algorithm 1. The estimation error term is controlled by a uniform concentration argument over theQ-我们的算法可以执行的类别和策略。特别地，每个迭代都属于策略类Π-class and over the policies that can be played by our algorithm. F.2 定理3.2的证明（奖励可实现性不足）F.2 Proof of Theorem 3.2 (Reward Realizability Is Insufficient)

到目前为止，我们表明，在So far, we showed that, underQπ_E-可实现性，交互式 IL 是可能的，但离线 IL 并非没有额外的覆盖范围。人们很自然地会问是否可以进一步削弱代表权要求。一个诱人的候选者是奖励的可实现性：当 _H-realizability, interactive IL is possible but offline IL is not without additional coverage. It is natural to ask whether one can weaken the representation requirement even further. A tempting candidate is reward realizability: when _H = 1, Q_π_E-可实现性降低为奖励可实现性，Joshi 等人。 （2026）表明奖励的可实现性足以满足上下文强盗的统计可学习性，尽管他们的算法在计算上效率低下。-realizability reduces to reward realizability, and Joshi et al. (2026) show that reward realizability is enough for statistical learnability in contextual bandits, although their algorithm is computationally inefficient.

下面的定理表明，在一般的 MDP 中，即使有交互，仅奖励可实现性也无法产生依赖于表示的保证：奖励类是单例，但学习器仍然需要覆盖罕见的决策状态。这激发了正文中使用的更强的假设，The theorem below shows that, in general MDPs, reward realizability alone cannot yield a representation-dependent guarantee even with interaction: the reward class is a singleton, but the learner still needs coverage of the rare decision states. This motivates the stronger assumption used in the main text,Q_π_E-可实现性，它存储有关奖励函数单独无法提供的转换的附加信息。-realizability, which stores additional information about the transitions that a reward function alone does not provide.

A deterministic greedy policy does as well when the maximizing action is unique. Other tie-breaking rules are covered only if they can be realized by an admissible sequence of VI scores. This gives a limiting connection to the roundwise greedy extraction step of Joshi et al. (2026); it does not assert that their final mixed output policy is itself a VI policy.

实践中使用的算法输出VI策略。Algorithms used in practice output VI policies.除了IL理论的先前工作（Moulin等人，2025b）之外，IL实践中使用的算法，例如IQ-LEARN（Garg等人，2021）和CSIL（Watson等人，2023），也是基于值的模仿算法。事实上，这些算法选择单个Beyond prior work in IL theory (Moulin et al., 2025b), algorithms used in practice in IL, such as IQ-LEARN (Garg et al., 2021) and CSIL (Watson et al., 2023), are also value-based imitation algorithms. 第二部分Part II

附加结果Additional Results

我们在这里收集了正文中省略的其他结果。我们首先使用 OVI 的变体完成第 3 节。在附录 H 中，我们展示了颠倒 OVI 中玩家更新的顺序会产生一种计算效率较低的算法，但为非凸提供了统计优势We collect here additional results omitted from the main text. We begin by completing Section 3 with a variant of OVI. In Appendix H, we show that reversing the order in which the players update in OVI yields an algorithm that is less computationally efficient, but provides statistical benefits for nonconvexQ类，并允许我们学习折扣 MDP 中的固定解决方案。classes and allows us to learn a stationary solution in discounted MDPs.

除了第 4 节中的下限之外，我们在附录一中表明，如果假设（最佳）专家涵盖了学习者的分布，则可以进行离线学习。In addition to the lower bound from Section 4, we show in Appendix I that it is possible to learn offline if one assumes the (optimal) expert covers the learner’s distribution.

此外，在附录 J 中，我们表明，即使课堂上互动也能实现高效学习Moreover, in Appendix J, we show that interaction enables efficient learning even when the classQ实现任一realizes eitherQπ_E或_Qor the _Q-算法可能产生的任何策略的值，而学习者不知道哪种情况成立。关键的算法思想是从专家和学习者占用率测量的混合中对状态进行采样，这种技术在语言模型的背景下被证明是成功的（Agarwal 等人，2024；Li 等人，2026a）。因此，OVI 的这种自适应变体适用于更大的类集-value of any policy that the algorithm might produce, without the learner knowing which case holds. The key algorithmic idea is to sample states from a mixture of the expert and learner occupancy measures, a technique proven successful in the context of language models (Agarwal et al., 2024; Li et al., 2026a). This adaptive variant of OVI therefore applies to a strictly larger set of classesQ比 Moulin 等人的离线算法所覆盖的范围大。 （2025a），揭示了交互的一个以前未知的好处。than that covered by the offline algorithm of Moulin et al. (2025a), revealing a previously unknown benefit of interaction.

尽管具有强大的统计保证，但当类很大或连续时，算法 3 在计算上并不有吸引力，因为覆盖集的构造很麻烦。我们比较Despite having strong statistical guarantees, Algorithm 3 is not computationally attractive when the class is large or continuous because the construction of the covering set is cumbersome. We compareQ-OVI 和 OVI 下面。-OVI and OVI below.

计算复杂度的比较。Comparison of the computational complexity.如前所述，算法 1 更适合实际实现，因为它不需要对类进行离散化As mentioned, Algorithm 1 is better suited for a practical implementation because it does not require discretizing the classQ。虽然我们认为线性情况可以变得高效，但这种方法显然在以下情况下不可扩展：. While we think that the linear case could be made efficient, this approach is clearly not scalable whenQ对应于现代建筑的权重类别。corresponds to the class of weights for modern architectures.Q-OVI 将学习一组指数级重量配置的分布。相比之下，OVI 与指定损失函数并通过它进行反向传播的常见做法兼容。-OVI would learn a distribution over a set of exponentially many weight configurations. In contrast, OVI is compatible with the common practice of specifying a loss function and backpropagating through it.

统计复杂性的比较。Comparison of the statistical complexity.上课的时候When the classQ是凸的，两种算法都达到了有序率is convex, both algorithms achieve a rate of orderO ( ε−_2）。在非凸情况下会出现速率差异，其中算法 3 的样本复杂度为 _O 级). A rate difference emerges in the nonconvex case, where Algorithm 3 has a sample complexity of order _O ( ε−_2），而算法 1 的 _O 不是最优的), while Algorithm 1 has a suboptimal _O ( ε_−_4）率。) rate.

总而言之，To summarize,Q-OVI（算法 3）具有更好的统计特性，但与 OVI（算法 1）相比，计算效率较低，因为它需要对类进行离散化-OVI (Algorithm 3) has better statistical properties but is computationally inefficient compared to OVI (Algorithm 1) because it requires discretizing the classQ .

I 用覆盖率突破线下下限I Breaking the Offline Lower Bound with Coverage

本节表明，当专家具有足够的探索性时，离线下限不适用，如通过下面假设 I.1 和 I.2 中引入的覆盖系数来衡量的。我们通过对 OVI 的概括分析证明了这一点。This section shows that the offline lower bound does not apply when the expert is sufficiently exploratory, as measured by a coverage coefficient introduced in Assumptions I.1 and I.2 below. J 论混合专家轨迹和学习者轨迹的好处J On the Benefits of Mixing Expert and Learner Trajectories

在本节中，我们分析了混合专家和学习者数据以创建 LM 蒸馏中的经验目标的流行技术（Agarwal 等人，2024；Li 等人，2026a），并表明它会带来代表性收益。我们开发了一种算法，当类In this section, we analyze the popular technique of mixing expert and learner data to create the empirical objective in LM distillation (Agarwal et al., 2024; Li et al., 2026a) and show that it leads to representational benefits. We develop an algorithm that is statistically efficient when the classQ满足任一satisfies eitherQπ_E-可实现性或_Q-realizability or the _QΠQ-Moulin等人提出的可实现性条件。 (2025a),-realizability condition introduced by Moulin et al. (2025a),不知道先验的表征条件成立without knowing a priori which representational condition holds .

为了获得本节中所需的随时后悔界限，我们使用略有不同的版本To obtain the anytime regret bounds needed in this section, we use a slightly different version of theQΠQ- 实现条件。与 Moulin 等人相比。 (2025a)，我们的版本使用 FTRL 而不是 OMD，并允许 softmax 权重取决于固定的学习率计划-realizability condition. Compared with Moulin et al. (2025a), our version uses FTRL rather than OMD and allows the softmax weights to depend on a fixed learning-rate scheduleη 1: K+1。+1.
